📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期乙型肝炎病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 在 ETV 停用者中,临床复发率随 EOT qHBsAg 升高而升高(<2、2-3 和 ≥3 log10 IU/mL 分别为 0.0%、14.6% 和 25.0%)。无论 EOT qHBsAg 水平如何,TAF 停药者均表现出较高的病毒学复发率 (68.8%–86.3%) 和临床复发率 (48.5%–57.1%)。在 2-3 log10 IU/mL 时,临床复发的次分布风险比(TAF 与 ETV)为 5.164 (95% CI 2.131-12.515),在 <2 log10 IU/mL 时没有 ETV 事件;总体 SHR 为 5.633 (95% CI 2.801–11.329;p < 0.001)。
  • 抗 HBs 滴度是可变的,并且会随着时间的推移而下降,并且检测不到抗 HBs 并不总是表明免疫记忆丧失或对 HBV 感染易感性。 [10.1111/pde.70337]。
  • 1995 年至 2022 年间,记录了 1,280 例乙型肝炎(55.5% 男性)和 6,658 例丙型肝炎(64.3% 男性)报告。总共观察到 145 例肝癌登记和 270 例失代偿性肝硬化相关入院病例。
  • 344 名有效受访者中,161 名提供了血液样本;HBsAg 阳性率为 11.80%,抗-HBs 阳性率为 60.87%,抗-HBe 阳性率为 23.60%,抗-HBc 阳性率为 77.02%,没有参与者的 HBeAg 检测呈阳性。乙肝知识总体知晓率为36.69%,合格率为22.09%。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Among 1203 incident HCC cases, 219 (18.2%) were HBV-related. Cirrhosis was present in 141 (64%) patients, with 57 (26%) newly diagnosed at HCC presentation.
  • Among ETV discontinuers, clinical relapse rates rose with higher EOT qHBsAg (0.0%, 14.6%, and 25.0% for <2, 2–3, and ≥3 log10 IU/mL, respectively).
  • Anti-HBs titers are variable and decline over time, and absence of detectable anti-HBs does not always indicate loss of immune memory or susceptibility to HBV infection. [10.1111/pde.70337].
  • Between 1995 and 2022, 1,280 hepatitis B (55.5% male) and 6,658 hepatitis C (64.3% male) notifications were recorded.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Journal of acquired immune deficiency syndromes (1999)  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-01  |  DOI: 10.1097/qai.0000000000003896  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Future scheduled publication  |  Citation: 2026, 101, 9, 964-972
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

HIV 治疗 网站的乙型和丙型肝炎预防、筛查和诊断服务:评估艾滋病的国际 流行病学 数据库。

Hepatitis B and C Prevention, Screening, and Diagnostic Services at HIV Treatment Sites: International Epidemiology Databases to Evaluate AIDS.
作者： Mark H Kuniholm, Gad Murenzi, Niharika Samala, Evy Yunihastuti, Gilles Wandeler, H Nina Kim, Marie K Plaisy, Hugo Perazzo, Christella Twizere, Francesca Odhiambo
摘要中文翻译背景:乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 的预防、筛查和诊断服务可以预防接受 HIV 护理的人群发病和 死亡率。然而,有关全球艾滋病毒诊所提供的乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎服务的信息有限。方法:国际 流行病学 艾滋病评估数据库 (IeDEA) 对来自 7 个地区的参与 HIV 治疗 中心的服务提供和实践进行了调查。我们使用 2023 年调查数据来衡量 HBV 疫苗接种、HBV 和 HCV 筛查、HBV 表面抗原 (HBsAg)、HBV DNA、HCV 抗体和 HCV RNA 检测的可用性。使用多变量逻辑回归模型来测试站点特征与 HBV 和 HCV 服务的关联。结果:204 个 HIV 治疗 站点中 67.7% 的 HBV 疫苗接种 可现场获得。据报告,72.1% 和 50% 的机构在 HIV 护理登记时进行了 HBV 和 HCV 筛查。HBsAg、HBV DNA、HCV 抗体和 HCV RNA 现场检测率分别为 77%、47.6%、61.8% 和 44.6%。主要服务于农村(相对于城市)人口的站点不太可能报告 HBV DNA 的现场可用性 [比值比 (OR):0.07;95%置信区间:0.01至0.68;P = 0.02]、HCV 抗体(OR = 0.18;95% CI:0.04 至 0.92;P = 0.04)和 HCV RNA(OR = 0.10;95% CI:0.01 至 0.90;P = 0.04)检测。结论:参与 IeDEA 网络的大多数 HIV 治疗 站点均可现场提供 HBV 疫苗接种、HBsAg 和 HCV 抗体检测等挽救生命的服务。农村地区的可用性较低表明,扩大服务范围对于消除乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎作为接受艾滋病毒护理的人群的公共卫生问题非常重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了以下公共卫生问题:尽管提供了有效的预防和诊断服务,但在接受艾滋病毒护理的人群中,乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 的发病率和 死亡率 仍然很高。作者发现了艾滋病诊所在全球范围内提供乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎服务方面的知识差距。
设计与对象
国际 流行病学 艾滋病评估数据库 (IeDEA) 于 2023 年对全球七个地区的 204 个 HIV 治疗 中心的服务提供和实践进行了横断面调查。
核心方法
该调查测量了 HBV 疫苗接种、HBV 和 HCV 筛查以及特定测试(HBsAg、HBV DNA、HCV 抗体、HCV RNA)的现场可用性。使用多变量逻辑回归模型来测试站点特征与 HBV 和 HCV 服务可用性之间的关联。
主要结果
67.7% 的研究中心可现场进行 HBV 疫苗接种,分别有 72.1% 和 50% 的研究中心报告在入组时进行了 HBV 和 HCV 筛查,分别有 77%、47.6%、61.8% 和 44.6% 的研究中心进行了 HBsAg、HBV DNA、HCV 抗体和 HCV RNA 现场检测。主要服务于农村人口的站点不太可能提供现场 HBV DNA(OR 0.07;95% CI 0.01-0.68;P=0.02)、HCV 抗体(OR 0.18;95% CI 0.04-0.92;P=0.04)和 HCV RNA(OR 0.10;95% CI 0.01-0.90;P=0.04)。P=0.04)测试。
解释与创新
作者对研究结果的解释是,大多数参与 HIV 治疗 的站点都可以现场提供挽救生命的 HBV 和 HCV 服务,但农村地区的可用性较低,凸显了可能阻碍消除工作的服务差距。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明,扩大乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎服务,特别是在农村地区,对于在接受艾滋病毒护理的人群中实现消除目标至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究的横断面设计和对自我报告调查数据的依赖限制了因果推断,并可能高估了服务的可用性;由于潜在的报告偏差和 IeDEA 网络站点之外的普遍性有限,证据强度中等。

Hepatitis B and C Prevention, Screening, and Diagnostic Services at HIV Treatment Sites: International Epidemiology Databases to Evaluate AIDS.

Authors: Mark H Kuniholm, Gad Murenzi, Niharika Samala, Evy Yunihastuti, Gilles Wandeler, H Nina Kim, Marie K Plaisy, Hugo Perazzo, Christella Twizere, Francesca Odhiambo
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Prevention, screening, and diagnostic services for hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV) can prevent morbidity and mortality in people receiving HIV care. However, there is limited information about the availability of HBV and HCV services at HIV clinics globally. METHODS: The International epidemiology Databases to Evaluate AIDS (IeDEA) conducted surveys of service delivery and practices at participating HIV treatment centers from 7 regions. We used 2023 survey data to measure availability of HBV vaccination, HBV and HCV screening, HBV surface antigen (HBsAg), HBV DNA, HCV antibody, and HCV RNA testing. Multivariable logistic regression models were used to test associations of site characteristics with HBV and HCV services. RESULTS: HBV vaccination was available on-site at 67.7% of 204 HIV treatment sites. Screening for HBV and HCV at HIV care enrollment was reported by 72.1% and 50% of sites, respectively. HBsAg, HBV DNA, HCV antibody, and HCV RNA testing were available on-site at 77%, 47.6%, 61.8%, and 44.6% of sites, respectively. Sites serving predominately rural (vs. urban) populations were less likely to report on-site availability of HBV DNA [odds ratio (OR):0.07; 95% confidence interval: 0.01 to 0.68; P = 0.02], HCV antibody (OR = 0.18; 95% CI: 0.04 to 0.92; P = 0.04), and HCV RNA (OR = 0.10; 95% CI: 0.01 to 0.90; P = 0.04) testing. CONCLUSION: Life-saving services such as HBV vaccination, HBsAg, and HCV antibody testing were available on-site at most HIV treatment sites participating in the IeDEA network. Lower availability at rural sites suggests that expansion of services is important to eliminate HBV and HCV as public health problems in people receiving HIV care.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the public-health problem that morbidity and mortality from hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV) remain high among people receiving HIV care, despite the availability of effective prevention and diagnostic services. The authors identified a knowledge gap regarding the global availability of HBV and HCV services at HIV clinics.
Design and population
The International epidemiology Databases to Evaluate AIDS (IeDEA) conducted a cross-sectional survey of service delivery and practices at 204 HIV treatment centers across seven global regions in 2023.
Core methods
The survey measured on-site availability of HBV vaccination, HBV and HCV screening, and specific tests (HBsAg, HBV DNA, HCV antibody, HCV RNA). Multivariable logistic regression models were used to test associations between site characteristics and the availability of HBV and HCV services.
Main results
HBV vaccination was available on-site at 67.7% of sites, screening for HBV and HCV at enrollment was reported by 72.1% and 50% of sites, respectively, and HBsAg, HBV DNA, HCV antibody, and HCV RNA testing were available on-site at 77%, 47.6%, 61.8%, and 44.6% of sites, respectively. Sites serving predominantly rural populations were less likely to offer on-site HBV DNA (OR 0.07; 95% CI 0.01–0.68; P=0.02), HCV antibody (OR 0.18; 95% CI 0.04–0.92; P=0.04), and HCV RNA (OR 0.10; 95% CI 0.01–0.90; P=0.04) testing.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as showing that life-saving HBV and HCV services were available on-site at most participating HIV treatment sites, but lower availability in rural settings highlights a service gap that could impede elimination efforts.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results suggest that expanding HBV and HCV services, particularly in rural areas, is critical to achieving elimination goals among people receiving HIV care.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s cross-sectional design and reliance on self-reported survey data limit causal inference and may overestimate service availability; the evidence strength is moderate due to potential reporting bias and limited generalizability beyond IeDEA network sites.

临床进展记录:乙型肝炎

Clinical progress note: Hepatitis B
作者： Yarlini Vipulanandan, Claudette L. Poole
摘要中文翻译摘要 乙型肝炎病毒 是世界范围内众所周知的急慢性肝病感染病因。值得注意的是,90% 在婴儿期感染乙型肝炎的儿童会发展为慢性感染并随后发展为进行性肝病,包括肝硬化和肝细胞癌。30年前实施通用婴儿传播后,美国围产期传播显着减少,亚洲各地的HBV 发病率也大幅减少,与乙型肝炎疫苗接种的广泛传播相一致。然而,随着联邦 疫苗 指南的不断变化以及对 疫苗 犹豫不决的稳步上升,对 流行病学、传播、乙型肝炎筛查和预防的全面了解对于儿科医疗保健提供者仍然很重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 是世界范围内众所周知的急慢性肝病感染病因。该研究强调了儿科医疗保健提供者了解 传播、传播、乙型肝炎筛查和预防的重要性。
设计与对象
该研究是一份临床进展报告,重点关注人类 乙型肝炎病毒 感染,特别是在儿科医疗保健方面。它讨论了 疫苗接种 计划的影响以及联邦 疫苗 指南的变化和 疫苗 犹豫所带来的持续挑战。
核心方法
所提供的证据中没有明确详细说明方法部分,但该研究参考了美国通用婴儿 传播 的实施及其对减少 传播 围产期 传播 的影响。
主要结果
该研究报告称,自 30 年前实施通用婴儿 传播 以来,美国围产期 传播 显着减少。它还注意到整个亚洲的 HBV 发病率 急剧减少,与广泛传播的乙型肝炎 发病率 一致。
解释与创新
作者解释说,疫苗接种 计划的成功导致了 乙型肝炎病毒 的 传播 和 发病率 的减少,但他们强调,由于 疫苗 指南的不断变化和犹豫不决,需要全面了解其 流行病学 和预防措施。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明了维持 疫苗接种 计划和了解 乙型肝炎病毒 的 流行病学 的重要性,以应对持续的公共卫生挑战,特别是在儿科人群中。
局限与证据强度
该研究没有提供详细的方法或统计分析,其结论是基于观察数据而不是对照实验。由于缺乏具体的统计细节,证据的强度中等。

Clinical progress note: Hepatitis B

Authors: Yarlini Vipulanandan, Claudette L. Poole
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Hepatitis B virus is a well‐known infectious etiology of acute and chronic liver disease worldwide. Notably, 90% of children infected with hepatitis B in infancy develop chronic infection and subsequent progressive liver disease, including cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma. Significant reduction in perinatal transmission in the United States (US) was seen after implementation of universal infant vaccination 30 years ago, as well as dramatic reductions in HBV incidence across Asia, coinciding with widespread hepatitis B vaccination. However, with ongoing changes to federal vaccine guidance and a steady rise in vaccine hesitancy, a comprehensive understanding of the epidemiology, transmission, screening, and prevention of hepatitis B remains important for pediatric health care providers.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus is a well‐known infectious etiology of acute and chronic liver disease worldwide. The study highlights the importance of understanding the epidemiology, transmission, screening, and prevention of hepatitis B for pediatric health care providers.
Design and population
The study is a clinical progress note focusing on hepatitis B virus infection in humans, particularly in the context of pediatric health care. It discusses the impact of vaccination programs and the ongoing challenges posed by changes in federal vaccine guidance and vaccine hesitancy.
Core methods
The methods section is not explicitly detailed in the provided evidence, but the study references the implementation of universal infant vaccination and its effect on reducing perinatal transmission of hepatitis B virus in the United States.
Main results
The study reports a significant reduction in perinatal transmission of hepatitis B virus in the United States following the implementation of universal infant vaccination 30 years ago. It also notes dramatic reductions in HBV incidence across Asia, coinciding with widespread hepatitis B vaccination.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the success of vaccination programs has led to reduced transmission and incidence of hepatitis B virus, but they emphasize the need for a comprehensive understanding of its epidemiology and prevention due to ongoing changes in vaccine guidance and rising hesitancy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the importance of maintaining vaccination programs and understanding the epidemiology of hepatitis B virus to address ongoing public health challenges, particularly in pediatric populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study does not provide detailed methods or statistical analyses, and its conclusions are based on observational data rather than controlled experiments. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the lack of specific statistical details.

Journal: Journal of viral hepatitis  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-01  |  DOI: 10.1111/jvh.70218  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Future scheduled publication  |  Citation: 2026, 33, 9, e70218
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

替诺福韦艾拉酚胺和恩替卡韦之间的乙型肝炎表面抗原聚糖异构体动力学差异:乙型肝炎 e 抗原阳性患者的肝细胞癌风险分层。

Differential Hepatitis B Surface Antigen Glycan Isomer Kinetics Between Tenofovir Alafenamide and Entecavir: Hepatocellular Carcinoma Risk Stratification in Hepatitis B e Antigen-Positive Patients.
作者： Yuji Kita, Ayato Murata, Hiroki Nago, Masahiro Yamaguchi, Yoko Kato, Rihwa Om, Yuichro Terai, Yuji Ikeda, Shunsuke Sato, Yuji Shimada
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管核(酸)类似物疗法可抑制 HBV DNA,但残留肝细胞癌 (HCC) 风险仍然存在,因此需要更好的风险分层标记。该研究询问 on-治疗 乙型肝炎表面抗原聚糖异构体 (HBsAgGi) 动力学是否可以区分 HCC 风险,以及替诺福韦艾拉酚胺 (TAF) 在调节这些动力学方面是否优于恩替卡韦 (ETV)。
设计与对象
这是一项前瞻性 队列研究 研究,纳入了 90 名未接受过 NA 的 慢性乙型肝炎 患者,开始使用 TAF (n=17) 或 ETV (n=73),并随访 48 周。该人群包括接受标准护理 抗病毒药物 治疗的 HBeAg 阳性和 HBeAg 阴性成年人。
核心方法
使用基因型 C 特异性 O-糖基化测定对 HBsAgGi 进行定量;有利的反应被定义为降低或维持低 HBsAgGi 水平。带有惩罚估计的逻辑回归评估了有利的 HBsAgGi 动力学的预测因子,Cox 模型评估了 HCC 风险关联。
主要结果
TAF independently predicted a favourable HBsAgGi response versus ETV (penalised odds ratio 3.60, p=0.030). In HBeAg-positive patients, poor HBsAgGi response was associated with increased HCC risk (hazard ratio 7.34, 95% CI 1.96–20.35, p=0.001), whereas no association was observed in HBeAg-negative patients.
解释与创新
作者将 治疗 HBsAgGi 反应解释为 HBeAg 阳性患者残留 HCC 风险的特异性替代标志物,并得出结论,TAF 比 ETV 诱导更有利的 HBsAgGi 动力学。这是第一份将 NA 治疗期间 HBsAgGi 动力学与 HCC 风险分层联系起来的报告。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,将 HBsAgGi 反应纳入现有 HCC 风险评分(例如 aMAP)可以完善 HBeAg 阳性患者的长期风险预测。它们还意味着 TAF 相对于 ETV 在通过改善 HBsAgGi 动力学降低 HCC 风险方面具有潜在优势。
局限与证据强度
C 基因型特异性检测限制了对其他 HBV 基因型的普遍适用性。HCC 风险比的宽置信区间需要谨慎解释,并且该研究适度的样本量和单中心设计限制了外部有效性。总体证据强度中等。

Differential Hepatitis B Surface Antigen Glycan Isomer Kinetics Between Tenofovir Alafenamide and Entecavir: Hepatocellular Carcinoma Risk Stratification in Hepatitis B e Antigen-Positive Patients.

Authors: Yuji Kita, Ayato Murata, Hiroki Nago, Masahiro Yamaguchi, Yoko Kato, Rihwa Om, Yuichro Terai, Yuji Ikeda, Shunsuke Sato, Yuji Shimada
Original abstract (English)
Nucleos(t)ide analog (NA) therapy suppresses hepatitis B virus (HBV) DNA, but residual hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) risk persists. O-glycosylated hepatitis B surface antigen glycan isomer (HBsAgGi) uniquely reflects virion burden. We evaluated the impact of tenofovir alafenamide (TAF) versus entecavir (ETV) on 48-week HBsAgGi kinetics and its utility in stratifying HCC risk in 90 NA-naïve patients (ETV: 73; TAF: 17). A favourable response was defined as a reduction or maintenance of low HBsAgGi levels. TAF independently predicted a favourable response compared with ETV (penalised odds ratio 3.60, p = 0.030). A significant interaction occurred between HBeAg status and HBsAgGi response in relation to HCC development (p = 0.038). In HBeAg-positive patients, a poor response was associated with increased HCC risk (hazard ratio 7.34, 95% confidence interval [CI] 1.96-20.35, p = 0.001); however, the exact magnitude warrants cautious interpretation due to wide CIs. This association was absent in HBeAg-negative cases. Adding HBsAgGi response to the aMAP score improved long-term HCC prediction exclusively in the HBeAg-positive cohort. As the assay targets genotype C-specific O-glycosylation, the findings cannot be generalised to genotypes A, B, or D. In conclusion, on-treatment HBsAgGi response is a specific surrogate marker for stratifying residual HCC risk in HBeAg-positive patients, and TAF is more effective than ETV in inducing these favourable kinetics.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Despite nucleos(t)ide analog therapy suppressing HBV DNA, residual hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) risk persists, creating a need for better risk-stratification markers. The study asked whether on-treatment hepatitis B surface antigen glycan isomer (HBsAgGi) kinetics could distinguish HCC risk and whether tenofovir alafenamide (TAF) outperforms entecavir (ETV) in modulating these kinetics.
Design and population
This was a prospective cohort study of 90 NA-naïve chronic hepatitis B patients initiating either TAF (n=17) or ETV (n=73) and followed for 48 weeks. The population comprised HBeAg-positive and HBeAg-negative adults receiving standard-of-care antiviral therapy.
Core methods
HBsAgGi was quantified using a genotype C-specific O-glycosylation assay; a favourable response was defined as reduction or maintenance of low HBsAgGi levels. Logistic regression with penalised estimation evaluated predictors of favourable HBsAgGi kinetics, and Cox models assessed HCC risk associations.
Main results
TAF independently predicted a favourable HBsAgGi response versus ETV (penalised odds ratio 3.60, p=0.030). In HBeAg-positive patients, poor HBsAgGi response was associated with increased HCC risk (hazard ratio 7.34, 95% CI 1.96–20.35, p=0.001), whereas no association was observed in HBeAg-negative patients.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret on-treatment HBsAgGi response as a specific surrogate marker for residual HCC risk in HBeAg-positive patients and conclude that TAF induces more favourable HBsAgGi kinetics than ETV. This is the first report linking HBsAgGi kinetics to HCC risk stratification during NA therapy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that incorporating HBsAgGi response into existing HCC risk scores (e.g., aMAP) could refine long-term risk prediction in HBeAg-positive patients. They also imply a potential advantage of TAF over ETV in reducing HCC risk via improved HBsAgGi kinetics.
Limitations and evidence strength
The genotype C-specific assay limits generalisability to other HBV genotypes. The wide confidence intervals around the HCC hazard ratio warrant cautious interpretation, and the study’s modest sample size and single-centre design constrain external validity. Overall evidence strength is moderate.

艾伯塔省 慢性乙型肝炎 和丁型肝炎检测的地理空间差异:基于人群的空间分析。

Geospatial Discrepancy of Chronic Hepatitis B and Hepatitis D Testing in Alberta: A Population-Based Spatial Analysis.
作者： Bryce Tkachuk, Isabelle Couloigner, Carla S Coffin, Jason Jiang, Stefania Bertazzon, Abdel-Aziz Shaheen
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 和丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 双重感染是肝硬化和肝细胞癌的主要原因。CHB 的地理分布以及抗 HDV 检测与 CHB 负担的一致性仍然不明确。
设计与对象
2014-2022年间,成年慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)患者通过省级实验室数据库(包含乙型肝炎病毒/丁型肝炎病毒血清学的血清学算法)进行验证性识别。病例被地理定位至聚合分发区域(ADAs),并与阿尔伯塔健康服务区域及城乡连续体等级关联。在8317名慢性乙型肝炎患者中,省级年龄和性别标准化患病率为1.70/1000人(95%置信区间,1.66-1.74)。
核心方法
省级年龄和性别标准化 患病率 是根据 ADA 使用 2016 年加拿大人口普查计算得出的。使用描述性映射、全局空间自相关和局部热点分析来评估空间聚类。总体而言,17.5% 的慢性乙型肝炎患者接受了抗 HDV 检测,其中 4.1% 的患者抗 HDV 呈阳性。
主要结果
CHB 患病率 热点集中在大都市 ADA,而抗 HDV 检测和阳性的热点仅与这些高负担地区部分重叠。艾伯塔省的慢性乙型肝炎负担在地理上集中,特别是在大都市的特定社区内,而抗丁型肝炎病毒检测仍然很少,并且仅部分与高负担地区一致。
解释与创新
该研究强调了慢性乙型肝炎的地理分布和抗 HDV 检测频率之间的差异,表明检测实践可能无法与慢性乙型肝炎高负担地区保持最佳一致。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,改善高负担地区的抗丁型肝炎病毒检测可以加强病例发现并为有针对性的公共卫生干预措施提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究由于依赖省级实验室数据以及偏远地区可能存在漏报的情况而受到限制。由于该研究的观察性质和缺乏干预数据,证据强度中等。

Geospatial Discrepancy of Chronic Hepatitis B and Hepatitis D Testing in Alberta: A Population-Based Spatial Analysis.

Authors: Bryce Tkachuk, Isabelle Couloigner, Carla S Coffin, Jason Jiang, Stefania Bertazzon, Abdel-Aziz Shaheen
Original abstract (English)
Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) and hepatitis D virus (HDV) coinfection are major causes of cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma. The geographic distribution of CHB and alignment of anti-HDV testing with CHB burden remain poorly defined. We aimed to examine if anti-HDV testing and seropositivity align with areas of highest CHB burden. Adults with CHB (2014-2022) were identified using a validated serologic algorithm in the provincial laboratory database capturing HBV/HDV serology. Cases were geolocated to Aggregate Dissemination Areas (ADAs), linked to Alberta Health Services zones and rural-urban continuum levels. Age- and sex-standardized CHB prevalence was calculated per ADA using the 2016 Canadian Census. Descriptive mapping, global spatial autocorrelation and local hotspot analysis were used to assess spatial clustering. Among 8317 persons with CHB, the provincial age- and sex-standardized prevalence was 1.70 per 1000 population (95% CI, 1.66-1.74). Prevalence ranged from 2.15 per 1000 in metropolitan areas to 0.40 per 1000 in remote regions and was higher in Calgary than Edmonton (2.53 vs. Overall, 17.5% of CHB patients were tested for anti-HDV, and 4.1% of those tested were anti-HDV positive. CHB prevalence hotspots were concentrated in metropolitan ADAs, whereas hotspots of anti-HDV testing and positivity showed only partial overlap with these high-burden areas. CHB burden in Alberta is geographically clustered, particularly within specific neighbourhoods of metropolitan cities, whereas anti-HDV testing remains infrequent and only partially aligned with high-burden areas. Reflex or systematic anti-HDV testing, paired with geographically informed outreach, may improve case detection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) and hepatitis D virus (HDV) coinfection are major causes of cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma. The geographic distribution of CHB and alignment of anti-HDV testing with CHB burden remain poorly defined.
Design and population
Adults with CHB (2014-2022) were identified using a validated serologic algorithm in the provincial laboratory database capturing HBV/HDV serology. Cases were geolocated to Aggregate Dissemination Areas (ADAs), linked to Alberta Health Services zones and rural-urban continuum levels. Among 8317 persons with CHB, the provincial age- and sex-standardized prevalence was 1.70 per 1000 population (95% CI, 1.66-1.74).
Core methods
The provincial age- and sex-standardized prevalence was calculated per ADA using the 2016 Canadian Census. Descriptive mapping, global spatial autocorrelation and local hotspot analysis were used to assess spatial clustering. Overall, 17.5% of CHB patients were tested for anti-HDV, and 4.1% of those tested were anti-HDV positive.
Main results
CHB prevalence hotspots were concentrated in metropolitan ADAs, whereas hotspots of anti-HDV testing and positivity showed only partial overlap with these high-burden areas. CHB burden in Alberta is geographically clustered, particularly within specific neighbourhoods of metropolitan cities, whereas anti-HDV testing remains infrequent and only partially aligned with high-burden areas.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights a discrepancy between the geographic distribution of CHB and the frequency of anti-HDV testing, suggesting that testing practices may not be optimally aligned with areas of high CHB burden.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that improving anti-HDV testing in high-burden areas could enhance case detection and inform targeted public health interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on provincial laboratory data and the potential for underreporting in remote regions. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the study and the lack of intervention data.

在赞比亚处理 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒感染的一线医护人员中验证肝炎护理点超声检查。

Validation of a Point-Of-Care Ultrasound for Hepatitis Among Front-Line Healthcare Workers Managing Chronic Hepatitis B Virus Infection in Zambia.
作者： Costanza Bertoni, Annie Kanunga, Edford Sinkala, Enock Syabbalo, Helen Chitundu, Alick Chikwezi, Carolyn Chibundi, Guy K Muula, Samuel Bosomprah, Nicolò Capra
摘要中文翻译为弥合资源有限环境中乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染患者护理实施中的差距,我们评估了一种新型肝脏床旁超声(POCUS)肝炎筛查方案(PUSH)在赞比亚一线非放射科医护人员中的准确性。在2024年3月至6月期间,于赞比亚卢萨卡大学教学医院,我们培训了4名护士和6名有HBV管理经验但无超声(US)经验的医生,采用PUSH方案,包括在三个窗口(上腹部、肋下和右侧肋间)观察肝脏,以识别提示肝硬化的特征及疑似肝细胞癌(HCC)的肝脏病变。随后,连续纳入仅患有乙型肝炎(PWHB)或HBV/HIV合并感染的成年参与者,进行PUSH检查,操作者对临床数据不知情。我们使用瞬时弹性成像(TE)作为参考标准(RST)评估PUSH对显著纤维化和肝硬化的准确性,并使用经验丰富放射技师进行的全面腹部超声作为可能HCC肝脏病变的RST。采用非参数法分析PUSH的受试者工作特征曲线(ROC),并按操作者类型(医生vs护士)、性别和HIV状态进行调整。在197名分析的PWHB中,69.5%正在接受HBV抗病毒药物治疗,27.9%为HBV/HIV合并感染。根据RST结果,17.7%的PWHB存在显著纤维化,11.6%存在肝硬化,3.5%存在肝脏病变。PUSH对显著纤维化的准确性较低,敏感性为8.3%(17.5-41.4),特异性为100.0%(96.1-100),曲线下面积(AUROC)为0.54(0.43-0.65);对肝硬化的准确性为中等,敏感性为60.9%(38.5-80.3),特异性为97.7%(94.2-99.4),AUROC为0.79(0.69-0.89);对肝脏病变的准确性较高,敏感性为85.7%(42.1-99.6),特异性为98.4%(95.5-99.7),AUROC为0.92(0.78-1.00)。在赞比亚,经过快速培训的前线护士和医生能够使用PUSH以中等准确性诊断肝硬化,以高准确性诊断肝脏病变,尽管性能估计的置信区间较宽。在缺乏实验室系统和TE的情况下,包含PUSH的肝脏POCUS可能有助于HBV管理。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由于诊断访问障碍,慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染在资源有限的环境中仍然管理不善,这促使一线医护人员需要准确的护理点工具。
设计与对象
2024 年 3 月至 6 月,在赞比亚卢萨卡大学教学医院,四名具有 HBV 管理经验但没有接受过超声培训的护士和六名医生接受了 PUSH 方案培训,并对连续患有慢性 HBV 单独感染或 HBV/HIV 合并感染的成人进行了护理点超声检查。
核心方法
PUSH 使用三个窗口(上腹、肋下、右经肋)评估肝硬化提示特征和可疑肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的病变,操作人员对临床数据不知情;根据纤维化/肝硬化的瞬时弹性成像和 HCC 的综合腹部超声评估准确性,并根据操作者类型、性别和 HIV 状况调整非参数 ROC 分析。
主要结果
197名参与者中,17.7%有明显纤维化,11.6%有肝硬化,3.5%有肝脏病变;PUSH 对显着纤维化的准确性较低(敏感性 8.3%,特异性 100.0%,AUROC 0.54),对肝硬化的准确性中等(敏感性 60.9%,特异性 97.7%,AUROC 0.79),对肝脏病变的准确性较高(敏感性 85.7%,特异性 98.4%,AUROC 0.92)。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,经过快速培训后,一线护士和医生可以使用 PUSH 以中等准确度诊断肝硬化,并以较高准确度诊断肝脏病变,并注意到性能估计的置信区间较宽。
科研与公卫意义
在缺乏实验室系统和瞬时弹性成像的环境中,包括 PUSH 在内的肝脏 POCUS 可能有助于 HBV 管理,有可能改善肝硬化和 HCC 的早期检测。
局限与证据强度
该研究样本量小、置信区间宽限制了精度,且训练队列研究较小,可能影响普适性;证据强度被评估为中等。

Validation of a Point-Of-Care Ultrasound for Hepatitis Among Front-Line Healthcare Workers Managing Chronic Hepatitis B Virus Infection in Zambia.

Authors: Costanza Bertoni, Annie Kanunga, Edford Sinkala, Enock Syabbalo, Helen Chitundu, Alick Chikwezi, Carolyn Chibundi, Guy K Muula, Samuel Bosomprah, Nicolò Capra
Original abstract (English)
To close gaps in implementing care of people with hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection in resource-limited settings, we evaluated the accuracy of a novel liver point-of-care ultrasound (POCUS) protocol for hepatitis (PUSH) among front-line non-radiology healthcare workers in Zambia. At University Teaching Hospital in Lusaka, Zambia, from March to June 2024, we trained four nurses and six physicians with experience in HBV management, but not in ultrasound (US), in the PUSH protocol, which includes visualizing the liver in three windows (epigastric, subcostal and right transcostal) to identify cirrhosis-suggestive features and liver lesions suspicious for hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC). Then, consecutive adult participants with chronic hepatitis B (PWHB) alone or HBV/HIV coinfection underwent PUSH with operators blinded to clinical data. We evaluated the accuracy of PUSH for significant fibrosis and cirrhosis using transient elastography (TE) as the reference standard test (RST) and for liver lesions that were possible HCC using a comprehensive abdominal ultrasound by an experienced radiographer as the RST. Nonparametric analysis of the receiver operating curve (ROC) for PUSH was adjusted for operator type (doctor vs. nurse), sex and HIV status. Among 197 PWHBs analysed, 69.5% were taking HBV antivirals and 27.9% had HBV/HIV. According to RSTs, 17.7% of PWHBs had significant fibrosis, 11.6% had cirrhosis and 3.5% had liver lesions. PUSH had low accuracy for significant fibrosis, with sensitivity of 8.3% (17.5-41.4), specificity of 100.0% (96.1-100) and an area under the curve (AUROC) of 0.54 (0.43-0.65), moderate accuracy for cirrhosis, with sensitivity of 60.9% (38.5-80.3), specificity of 97.7% (94.2-99.4) and AUROC of 0.79 (0.69-0.89), and higher accuracy for liver lesions, with 85.7% (42.1-99.6) sensitivity, 98.4% (95.5-99.7) specificity, AUROC of 0.92 (0.78-1.00). After an expedited training, front-line nurses and physicians treating HBV in Zambia were able to use PUSH to diagnose cirrhosis with moderate accuracy and liver lesions with high accuracy, although confidence intervals around performance estimates were wide. Liver POCUS including PUSH may be useful in HBV management when laboratory systems and TE are lacking.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains under-managed in resource-limited settings due to diagnostic access barriers, prompting the need for accurate, point-of-care tools for front-line healthcare workers.
Design and population
At University Teaching Hospital in Lusaka, Zambia, from March to June 2024, four nurses and six physicians with HBV management experience but no ultrasound training were trained in the PUSH protocol and performed point-of-care ultrasound on consecutive adults with chronic HBV alone or HBV/HIV coinfection.
Core methods
PUSH assessed liver cirrhosis-suggestive features and lesions suspicious for hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) using three windows (epigastric, subcostal, right transcostal), with operators blinded to clinical data; accuracy was evaluated against transient elastography for fibrosis/cirrhosis and comprehensive abdominal ultrasound for HCC, with nonparametric ROC analysis adjusted for operator type, sex and HIV status.
Main results
Among 197 participants, 17.7% had significant fibrosis, 11.6% had cirrhosis and 3.5% had liver lesions; PUSH showed low accuracy for significant fibrosis (sensitivity 8.3%, specificity 100.0%, AUROC 0.54), moderate accuracy for cirrhosis (sensitivity 60.9%, specificity 97.7%, AUROC 0.79) and high accuracy for liver lesions (sensitivity 85.7%, specificity 98.4%, AUROC 0.92).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors conclude that after expedited training, front-line nurses and physicians could use PUSH to diagnose cirrhosis with moderate accuracy and liver lesions with high accuracy, noting wide confidence intervals around performance estimates.
Scientific and public-health significance
Liver POCUS including PUSH may be useful for HBV management in settings lacking laboratory systems and transient elastography, potentially improving early detection of cirrhosis and HCC.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s small sample size and wide confidence intervals limit precision, and the training cohort was small, which may affect generalisability; evidence strength is assessed as moderate.

在非肝硬化 HBeAg 阴性 慢性乙型肝炎 中,替诺福韦艾拉酚胺的治疗后复发风险高于恩替卡韦:治疗 结束时 qHBsAg 分层的见解

Higher Off-Therapy Relapse Risk with Tenofovir Alafenamide than Entecavir in Non-cirrhotic HBeAg-Negative Chronic Hepatitis B: Insights from End-of-Treatment qHBsAg Stratification
作者： Yi-Jie Huang, Chung-Hsin Chang, Shou-Wu Lee, Chun-Fang Tung, Szu-Chia Liao, Teng-Yu Lee, Yen-Chun Peng, Jun-Sing Wang
摘要中文翻译背景和目的:治疗 (EOT) 结束时 qHBsAg 可预测 HBeAg 阴性 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 的治疗后复发,但其分层能力在恩替卡韦 (ETV) 和替诺福韦艾拉酚胺 (TAF) 之间是否存在差异仍不清楚。本研究比较了非肝硬化 HBeAg 阴性 CHB 患者停止 ETV 或 TAF 后 12 个月的 EOT qHBsAg 复发风险。方法:在这项回顾性 队列研究 研究中,对停止 ETV 或 TAF 的非肝硬化 HBeAg 阴性 CHB 患者进行 1:1 倾向评分匹配。通过Kaplan-Meier分析和Cox回归评估病毒学复发;通过累积 发病率 函数与 Fine-Gray 竞争风险回归的临床复发。比较 EOT qHBsAg 分层(< 2、2-3、≥3 log10 IU/mL)的风险比和亚分布风险比 (sHR)。结果:总共分析了 130 名患者(每组 65 名)。在 ETV 停用者中,临床复发率随着 EOT qHBsAg 的升高而逐渐增加(<2、2-3 和 ≥3 log10 IU/mL 分别为 0.0%、14.6% 和 25.0%)。相比之下,无论 EOT qHBsAg 水平如何,TAF 停药者都表现出持续较高的病毒学复发率 (68.8%-86.3%) 和临床复发率 (48.5%-57.1%)。复发风险差异在较低 EOT qHBsAg 时最为明显:在 2-3 log10 IU/mL 时,临床复发的 sHR(TAF 与 ETV)达到 5.164 (95% CI 2.131-12.515),在 <2 log10 IU/mL 时没有 ETV 事件。总体 sHR 为 5.633 (95% CI 2.801-11.329;p < 0.001)。结论:EOT qHBsAg 有效地对 ETV 停用后的临床复发风险进行分层,但在 TAF 停用后显示出有限的区分能力,所有 qHBsAg 水平的复发率仍然一致较高。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了为什么在 HBeAg 阴性 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者中,恩替卡韦 (ETV) 和替诺福韦艾拉酚胺 (TAF) 之间停药后复发风险的 治疗 定量乙型肝炎表面抗原 (qHBsAg) 分层存在差异。其目的是澄清 EOT qHBsAg 是否在这些疗法中保持一致的预测价值。
设计与对象
这项回顾性 队列研究 研究使用 1:1 倾向评分匹配比较了非肝硬化 HBeAg 阴性 CHB 患者停止 ETV 或 TAF 后 12 个月的复发风险,每个 治疗 组有 65 名患者。
核心方法
通过Kaplan-Meier分析和Cox回归评估病毒学复发,而使用累积发病率函数和Fine-Gray竞争风险回归评估临床复发。比较了 EOT qHBsAg 分层(<2、2-3、≥3 log10 IU/mL)的风险比和亚分布风险比。
主要结果
在 ETV 停用者中,临床复发率随 EOT qHBsAg 升高而升高(<2、2-3 和 ≥3 log10 IU/mL 分别为 0.0%、14.6% 和 25.0%)。无论 EOT qHBsAg 水平如何,TAF 停药者均表现出较高的病毒学复发率 (68.8%–86.3%) 和临床复发率 (48.5%–57.1%)。在 2-3 log10 IU/mL 时,临床复发的次分布风险比(TAF 与 ETV)为 5.164 (95% CI 2.131-12.515),在 <2 log10 IU/mL 时没有 ETV 事件;总体 SHR 为 5.633 (95% CI 2.801–11.329;p < 0.001)。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,EOT qHBsAg 有效地对 ETV 停用后的临床复发风险进行了分层,但在 TAF 停用后显示出有限的区分能力,其中所有 qHBsAg 水平的复发率仍然一致较高。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,EOT qHBsAg 监测可以指导安全的 ETV 停药策略,而 TAF 停药则需要替代的风险分层方法,因为无论 qHBsAg 水平如何,复发风险持续较高。
局限与证据强度
回顾性设计、适度的样本量(n=130)和单中心设置限制了普遍性,并可能引入选择偏差;由于这些限制和缺乏外部验证,证据被评估为中等。

Higher Off-Therapy Relapse Risk with Tenofovir Alafenamide than Entecavir in Non-cirrhotic HBeAg-Negative Chronic Hepatitis B: Insights from End-of-Treatment qHBsAg Stratification

Authors: Yi-Jie Huang, Chung-Hsin Chang, Shou-Wu Lee, Chun-Fang Tung, Szu-Chia Liao, Teng-Yu Lee, Yen-Chun Peng, Jun-Sing Wang
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND AND AIMS: End-of-treatment (EOT) qHBsAg predicts off-therapy relapse in HBeAg-negative chronic hepatitis B (CHB), but whether its stratifying ability differs between entecavir (ETV) and tenofovir alafenamide (TAF) remains unclear. This study compared 12-month relapse risk across EOT qHBsAg strata after ETV or TAF discontinuation in non-cirrhotic HBeAg-negative CHB patients. METHODS: In this retrospective cohort study, non-cirrhotic HBeAg-negative CHB patients discontinuing ETV or TAF were 1:1 propensity score matched. Virological relapse was assessed by Kaplan-Meier analysis and Cox regression; clinical relapse by cumulative incidence functions with Fine-Gray competing risks regression. Hazard ratios and subdistribution hazard ratios (sHRs) were compared across EOT qHBsAg strata (< 2, 2-3, ≥3 log10 IU/mL). RESULTS: A total of 130 patients (65 per group) were analyzed. Among ETV discontinuers, clinical relapse rates increased progressively with higher EOT qHBsAg (0.0%, 14.6%, and 25.0% for <2, 2-3, and ≥3 log10 IU/mL, respectively). In contrast, TAF discontinuers showed persistently high virological (68.8%-86.3%) and clinical relapse rates (48.5%-57.1%) regardless of EOT qHBsAg level. The relapse risk difference was most pronounced at lower EOT qHBsAg: the sHR for clinical relapse (TAF vs. ETV) reached 5.164 (95% CI 2.131-12.515) at 2-3 log10 IU/mL, with no ETV events at <2 log10 IU/mL. Overall sHR was 5.633 (95% CI 2.801-11.329; p < 0.001). CONCLUSIONS: EOT qHBsAg effectively stratifies clinical relapse risk after ETV discontinuation but shows limited discriminative ability after TAF cessation, where relapse rates remain uniformly high across all qHBsAg levels.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed why end-of-treatment quantitative hepatitis B surface antigen (qHBsAg) stratification for relapse risk after nucleos(t)ide analogue discontinuation differs between entecavir (ETV) and tenofovir alafenamide (TAF) in HBeAg-negative chronic hepatitis B (CHB). It aimed to clarify whether EOT qHBsAg retains predictive value uniformly across these therapies.
Design and population
This retrospective cohort study compared 12-month relapse risk after ETV or TAF discontinuation in non-cirrhotic HBeAg-negative CHB patients using 1:1 propensity score matching, yielding 65 patients per treatment group.
Core methods
Virological relapse was assessed by Kaplan-Meier analysis and Cox regression, while clinical relapse was evaluated using cumulative incidence functions with Fine-Gray competing risks regression. Hazard ratios and subdistribution hazard ratios were compared across EOT qHBsAg strata (<2, 2–3, ≥3 log10 IU/mL).
Main results
Among ETV discontinuers, clinical relapse rates rose with higher EOT qHBsAg (0.0%, 14.6%, and 25.0% for <2, 2–3, and ≥3 log10 IU/mL, respectively). TAF discontinuers showed uniformly high virological (68.8%–86.3%) and clinical relapse rates (48.5%–57.1%) regardless of EOT qHBsAg level. The subdistribution hazard ratio for clinical relapse (TAF vs. ETV) was 5.164 (95% CI 2.131–12.515) at 2–3 log10 IU/mL, with no ETV events at <2 log10 IU/mL; overall sHR was 5.633 (95% CI 2.801–11.329; p < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that EOT qHBsAg effectively stratifies clinical relapse risk after ETV discontinuation but shows limited discriminative ability after TAF cessation, where relapse rates remain uniformly high across all qHBsAg levels.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that EOT qHBsAg monitoring may guide safe ETV discontinuation strategies, whereas TAF cessation requires alternative risk-stratification approaches due to persistently high relapse risk irrespective of qHBsAg levels.
Limitations and evidence strength
The retrospective design, modest sample size (n=130), and single-centre setting limit generalisability and may introduce selection bias; the evidence is assessed as moderate due to these constraints and the absence of external validation.

肝动脉多普勒血流动力学指数作为 慢性乙型肝炎 成人疾病活动的成像生物标志物:与病毒载量和血清丙氨酸转氨酶的相关性

Hepatic Artery Doppler Haemodynamic Indices as Imaging Biomarkers of Disease Activity in Adults with Chronic Hepatitis B: Correlation with Viral Load and Serum Alanine Transaminase
作者： Abbas Rabiu Muhammad, Mohammed Abba Suwaid, Yusuf Lawal, Anas Ismail
摘要中文翻译摘要背景:乙型肝炎是慢性肝病的常见原因。使用超声和多普勒成像可以检测纤维化的早期特征。目的:本研究评估了乙型肝炎血清阳性成人与匹配的健康对照者的肝动脉多普勒参数、血清丙氨酸转氨酶 (ALT) 和 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 脱氧核糖核酸 (DNA) 定量(病毒载量)之间的关系。材料和方法:一项比较横断面研究评估了 143 名无症状乙型肝炎阳性成人和匹配对照者的灰度肝脏超声、肝动脉多普勒指数、ALT 和病毒载量。测量肝脏大小、回声纹理、阻力指数 (RI)、搏动指数 (PI)、ALT 和 HBV DNA 水平。使用平均值、中位数和标准差来总结数据。卡方和 t 检验比较各组,而 Pearson 相关性评估 ALT、RI 和 PI 之间的关系,显着性为 P < 0.05。结果:该研究将 143 名 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者与 143 名健康对照者进行了比较。肝跨度(13.0 厘米与 12.8 厘米)或回声性没有观察到显着差异,但乙型肝炎患者的肝动脉多普勒指数显着升高(RI 为 0.69 与 0.61,RI 为 1.25 与 1.25)。治疗 患者中 ALT 水平和可检测到的病毒载量升高更为常见。多普勒指数与 ALT 和病毒载量显着相关,表明它们在监测乙型肝炎进展方面具有潜在作用。结论:多普勒超声仍然是评估无症状 CHB 阳性患者肝血管变化的有价值的非侵入性工具,其 RI 和 PI 揭示了这些变化与乙型肝炎阳性患者血清 ALT 异常和病毒载量水平升高显着相关。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估乙型肝炎血清阳性成人与匹配的健康对照者的肝动脉多普勒参数、血清丙氨酸转氨酶 (ALT) 和 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) DNA 定量之间的关系。乙型肝炎是慢性肝病的常见原因,纤维化的早期特征可以通过超声和​​多普勒成像来检测。
设计与对象
一项比较横断面研究评估了 143 名无症状乙型肝炎阳性成人和匹配对照者的灰度肝脏超声、肝动脉多普勒指数、ALT 和病毒载量。该研究将 143 名 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者与 143 名健康对照者进行了比较。
核心方法
测量肝脏大小、回声纹理、阻力指数 (RI)、搏动指数 (PI)、ALT 和 HBV DNA 水平。使用平均值、中位数和标准差来总结数据。卡方和 t 检验比较各组,而 Pearson 相关性评估 ALT、RI 和 PI 之间的关系,显着性为 P < 0.05。
主要结果
肝跨度(13.0 厘米与 12.8 厘米)或回声性没有观察到显着差异,但乙型肝炎患者的肝动脉多普勒指数显着升高(RI 为 0.69 与 0.61,RI 为 1.25 与 1.25)。
解释与创新
多普勒指数与乙型肝炎阳性患者血清 ALT 异常和病毒载量水平升高显着相关,表明其在监测乙型肝炎进展方面具有潜在作用。
科研与公卫意义
服用 治疗 的患者中 ALT 水平升高和可检测到的病毒载量更常见,这表明监测这些参数对于疾病管理的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其横断面设计和对仅摘要证据的依赖而受到限制,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。由于缺乏长期随访和详细的临床结果,证据强度中等。

Hepatic Artery Doppler Haemodynamic Indices as Imaging Biomarkers of Disease Activity in Adults with Chronic Hepatitis B: Correlation with Viral Load and Serum Alanine Transaminase

Authors: Abbas Rabiu Muhammad, Mohammed Abba Suwaid, Yusuf Lawal, Anas Ismail
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background: Hepatitis B is a common cause of chronic liver disease. Early features of fibrosis are detectable using ultrasound and Doppler imaging. Aims: This study evaluated relationships between hepatic artery Doppler parameters, serum alanine transaminase (ALT) and hepatitis B virus (HBV) deoxyribonucleic acid (DNA) quantification (viral load) in seropositive adults with hepatitis B compared with matched healthy controls. Materials and Methods: A comparative cross-sectional study assessed grayscale liver ultrasound, hepatic artery Doppler indices, ALT and viral load among 143 asymptomatic hepatitis B-positive adults and matched controls. Liver size, echotexture, resistive index (RI), pulsatility index (PI), ALT and HBV DNA levels were measured. Data were summarised using mean, median and standard deviation. Chi-square and t -tests compared groups, while Pearson correlation assessed relationships among ALT, RI and PI, with significance at P < 0.05. Results: The study compared 143 chronic hepatitis B (CHB) patients with 143 healthy controls. No significant differences were observed in liver span (13.0 vs. 12.8 cm) or echogenicity, but hepatic artery Doppler indices were significantly elevated in hepatitis B patients (0.69 vs. 0.61 for RI and 1.25 vs. Elevated ALT levels and detectable viral loads were more common in patients on treatment. Doppler indices correlated significantly with ALT and the viral load, suggesting their potential role in monitoring hepatitis B progression. Conclusion: Doppler ultrasound remains a valuable non-invasive tool for assessing hepatic vascular changes among asymptomatic CHB-positive patients with RI and PI revealing those changes. Doppler indices correlated significantly with abnormal serum ALT and elevated viral load levels among hepatitis B-positive patients.

Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the relationships between hepatic artery Doppler parameters, serum alanine transaminase (ALT) and hepatitis B virus (HBV) DNA quantification in seropositive adults with hepatitis B compared with matched healthy controls. Hepatitis B is a common cause of chronic liver disease, and early features of fibrosis are detectable using ultrasound and Doppler imaging.

A comparative cross-sectional study assessed grayscale liver ultrasound, hepatic artery Doppler indices, ALT and viral load among 143 asymptomatic hepatitis B-positive adults and matched controls. The study compared 143 chronic hepatitis B (CHB) patients with 143 healthy controls.

Liver size, echotexture, resistive index (RI), pulsatility index (PI), ALT and HBV DNA levels were measured. Data were summarised using mean, median and standard deviation. Chi-square and t-tests compared groups, while Pearson correlation assessed relationships among ALT, RI and PI, with significance at P < 0.05.
Main results
No significant differences were observed in liver span (13.0 vs. 12.8 cm) or echogenicity, but hepatic artery Doppler indices were significantly elevated in hepatitis B patients (0.69 vs. 0.61 for RI and 1.25 vs.
Interpretation and novelty
Doppler indices correlated significantly with abnormal serum ALT and elevated viral load levels among hepatitis B-positive patients, suggesting their potential role in monitoring hepatitis B progression.
Scientific and public-health significance
Elevated ALT levels and detectable viral loads were more common in patients on treatment, indicating the importance of monitoring these parameters for disease management.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and reliance on abstract-only evidence, which may affect the generalisability of findings. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the absence of long-term follow-up and detailed clinical outcomes.

级联护理中的差距导致乙型肝炎患者肝癌预后不佳:一项多中心观察性 队列研究 研究。

Gaps in the Cascade of Care Contribute to Poor Liver Cancer Outcomes Among People Living With Hepatitis B: A Multicenter Observational Cohort Study.
作者： Dina Moussa, Joan Ericka Flores, Michael Macisaac, Jack Wallace, Joseph Doyle, Marno Ryan, Thai Hong, Brett Knowles, Adrian Fox, Ashu Jhamb
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管有有效的 抗病毒药物 疗法,但 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染仍然是澳大利亚肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的主要原因。该研究通过检查受影响个体的护理级联和临床特征,解决了理解为什么乙型肝炎相关的肝癌结果仍然不佳的差距。
设计与对象
在澳大利亚维多利亚州的八个三级卫生网络中进行了一项多中心观察性 队列研究 研究。回顾性地确定了2018年1月1日至2019年12月31日期间发生的HCC病例,前瞻性地确定了2020年1月1日至2022年10月17日期间发生的HCC病例。
核心方法
主要结局是由 HBV 引起的 HCC 事件比例,次要结局包括采用基于指南的 抗病毒药物 疗法和 监测 以及总生存期。使用多变量逻辑回归来确定与 治疗 和 监测 摄取相关的因素。
主要结果
Among 1203 incident HCC cases, 219 (18.2%) were HBV-related. Cirrhosis was present in 141 (64%) patients, with 57 (26%) newly diagnosed at HCC presentation. Linkage to specialist care was associated with higher treatment uptake (adjusted OR 8.4, 95% CI 4.3–16.1; p < 0.001).
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为 抗病毒药物 治疗 和肝硬化认知方面的差距持续存在的证据,导致 HCC 结局不佳。他们强调,错失预防和早期发现的机会是关键问题。
科研与公卫意义
该研究强调需要采取干预措施来改善护理和基于指南的乙肝治疗的联系,以减少乙肝相关的肝癌负担。它为高危人群中的 监测 和 治疗 策略提供了可行的见解。
局限与证据强度
回顾性-前瞻性设计和对三级卫生网络的依赖可能会限制其对其他环境的普遍适用性。由于潜在的选择偏差和护理级联指标中未测量的混杂因素,证据强度中等。

Gaps in the Cascade of Care Contribute to Poor Liver Cancer Outcomes Among People Living With Hepatitis B: A Multicenter Observational Cohort Study.

Authors: Dina Moussa, Joan Ericka Flores, Michael Macisaac, Jack Wallace, Joseph Doyle, Marno Ryan, Thai Hong, Brett Knowles, Adrian Fox, Ashu Jhamb
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) remains a major cause of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) in Australia, despite the availability of effective, subsidized antiviral therapy. We aimed to describe the clinical characteristics, HBV care cascade, and outcomes among people with HBV-related HCC in Victoria, Australia. METHODS: We conducted a multicenter cohort study of all incident HCC cases across eight tertiary health networks in Victoria, Australia. Cases were identified retrospectively (January 1, 2018 to December 31, 2019) and prospectively (January 1, 2020 to October 17, 2022). The primary outcome was the proportion of incident HCC attributable to HBV. Secondary outcomes included uptake of guideline-based antiviral therapy and surveillance and overall survival from HCC diagnosis. Multivariable logistic regression was used to identify factors associated with treatment and surveillance uptake. RESULTS: Among 1203 incident HCC cases, 219 (18.2%) were HBV related. Most patients were male (89%) and of Asian ethnicity (60%). While 61% were aged 56-75 years, 25% were 36-55 years. Cirrhosis was present in 141 (64%), although 57 (26%) were newly diagnosed with cirrhosis at HCC presentation. The 121 (60%) patients were eligible for antiviral therapy. Linkage to specialist care was associated with treatment uptake (adjusted OR 8.4, 95% CI 4.3-16.1; p < 0.001). Surveillance enrolment at diagnosis was higher among HBV-related HCC compared with non-HBV HCC (48% vs. CONCLUSION: HBV remains a substantial contributor to HCC in Australia. Gaps in antiviral treatment and cirrhosis recognition persist, highlighting missed opportunities for prevention and early detection. Interventions to improve linkage to care and delivery of guideline-based HBV management are urgently needed.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a leading cause of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) in Australia despite the availability of effective antiviral therapy. The study addressed gaps in understanding why HBV-related HCC outcomes remain poor by examining the care cascade and clinical characteristics of affected individuals.
Design and population
A multicenter observational cohort study was conducted across eight tertiary health networks in Victoria, Australia. Incident HCC cases were identified retrospectively from January 1, 2018 to December 31, 2019 and prospectively from January 1, 2020 to October 17, 2022.
Core methods
The primary outcome was the proportion of incident HCC attributable to HBV, with secondary outcomes including uptake of guideline-based antiviral therapy and surveillance and overall survival. Multivariable logistic regression was used to identify factors associated with treatment and surveillance uptake.
Main results
Among 1203 incident HCC cases, 219 (18.2%) were HBV-related. Cirrhosis was present in 141 (64%) patients, with 57 (26%) newly diagnosed at HCC presentation. Linkage to specialist care was associated with higher treatment uptake (adjusted OR 8.4, 95% CI 4.3–16.1; p < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as evidence that gaps in antiviral treatment and cirrhosis recognition persist, contributing to poor HCC outcomes. They highlight missed opportunities for prevention and early detection as key issues.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study underscores the need for interventions to improve linkage to care and delivery of guideline-based HBV management to reduce HBV-related HCC burden. It provides actionable insights for surveillance and treatment strategies in high-risk populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The retrospective-prospective design and reliance on tertiary health networks may limit generalisability to other settings. The evidence strength is moderate due to potential selection bias and unmeasured confounders in care cascade metrics.

死亡率 预测 5 年内乙型肝炎相关肝硬化门静脉血栓形成的新列线图

A New Nomogram for Mortality Prediction Within 5 Years in Portal Vein Thrombosis due to Hepatitis B‐Related Cirrhosis
作者： Yang Zhou, Feixiang Xiong, Jialiang Chen, Xiaoli Liu, Peipei Meng, Tong Wu, Hao Yu, Xiaomin Ji, Yuyong Jiang, Yixin Hou
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在开发列线图模型,用于预测伴有门静脉血栓 (PVT) 的乙型肝炎相关肝硬化患者的长期 死亡率。这项研究的动机是在特定的临床背景下需要改进 死亡率 预测工具。
设计与对象
2015年1月至2023年12月共招募961名患者,形成训练队列研究。该研究的重点是患有乙型肝炎相关肝硬化并发 PVT 的患者。
核心方法
通过COX回归分析确定了影响5年死亡率的独立危险因素,并根据这些因素构建了列线图。使用受试者工作特征 (ROC) 曲线、C 指数、校准曲线和决策曲线分析 (DCA) 评估列线图的效用。
主要结果
训练 队列研究 中列线图的 AUROC 值为 0.906 (95% CI: 0.891–0.921),验证 队列研究 中的 AUROC 值为 0.910 (95% CI: 0.885–0.927),与 CTP 和 MELD 评分等当前模型相比,表现出更好的性能和辨别能力。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为,与现有模型相比,列线图为预测乙型肝炎相关肝硬化和 PVT 患者的 5 年 死亡率 提供了更准确、更可靠的方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为乙型肝炎相关肝硬化和门静脉血栓患者开发更精确的 死亡率 预测工具提供信息,从而有可能改善临床决策和患者管理。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对单个 队列研究 的依赖以及可能存在未测量的混杂因素而受到限制。由于缺乏外部验证且使用单一数据集,证据强度中等。

A New Nomogram for Mortality Prediction Within 5 Years in Portal Vein Thrombosis due to Hepatitis B‐Related Cirrhosis

Authors: Yang Zhou, Feixiang Xiong, Jialiang Chen, Xiaoli Liu, Peipei Meng, Tong Wu, Hao Yu, Xiaomin Ji, Yuyong Jiang, Yixin Hou
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT This study aims to develop a nomogram model intended for predicting long‐term mortality in patients suffering from hepatitis B‐related cirrhosis associated with Portal vein thrombosis (PVT). A total of 961 patients were recruited from January 2015 to December 2023, forming the training cohort. We analysed independent risk factors affecting 5‐year mortality through COX regression, from which we constructed a nomogram. To assess the nomogram's utility, we employed the receiver operating characteristic (ROC) curve, C‐index, calibration curves, and decision curve analysis (DCA). The independent prognostic factors retained in the final model for patients with hepatitis B‐related cirrhosis complicated by PVT included age (HR = 1.09, 95% CI: 1.06–1.11; p < 0.001), leukocyte count (WBC) (HR = 1.09, 95% CI: 1.03–1.17; p = 0.002), and the neutrophil‐to‐lymphocyte ratio (NLR) (HR = 1.29, 95% CI: 1.22–1.35; p < 0.001), along with serum sodium (HR = 0.94, 95% CI: 0.90–0.98; p = 0.005). The AUROC values for the nomogram were 0.906 (95% CI: 0.891–0.921) in the training cohort and 0.910 (95% CI: 0.885–0.927) in the validation cohort, demonstrating improved performance and discriminatory ability compared to current models, including CTP and MELD scores. Age, leukocyte count (WBC), NLR, and serum sodium emerged as independent risk factors impacting prognosis for patients with hepatitis B cirrhosis and PVT within 5 years. The nomogram developed in this research holds significant potential for accurately predicting the outcomes of these patients within 5 years.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to develop a nomogram model for predicting long-term mortality in patients with hepatitis B-related cirrhosis associated with Portal vein thrombosis (PVT). The research was motivated by the need for improved mortality prediction tools in this specific clinical context.
Design and population
A total of 961 patients were recruited from January 2015 to December 2023, forming the training cohort. The study focused on patients suffering from hepatitis B-related cirrhosis complicated by PVT.
Core methods
Independent risk factors affecting 5-year mortality were identified through COX regression analysis, and a nomogram was constructed based on these factors. The nomogram's utility was assessed using receiver operating characteristic (ROC) curve, C-index, calibration curves, and decision curve analysis (DCA).
Main results
The AUROC values for the nomogram were 0.906 (95% CI: 0.891–0.921) in the training cohort and 0.910 (95% CI: 0.885–0.927) in the validation cohort, showing improved performance and discriminatory ability compared to current models like CTP and MELD scores.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating that the nomogram provides a more accurate and reliable method for predicting 5-year mortality in patients with hepatitis B-related cirrhosis and PVT compared to existing models.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more precise mortality prediction tools for patients with hepatitis B-related cirrhosis and PVT, potentially improving clinical decision-making and patient management.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on a single cohort and the potential for unmeasured confounding factors. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the absence of external validation and the use of a single dataset.

衢州市乙型肝炎报告病例配偶乙型肝炎血清流行率及其知识知晓情况的横断面研究

Hepatitis B seroprevalence and knowledge awareness among spouses of reported Hepatitis B cases in Quzhou city: A cross-sectional study
作者： Wangfeng Zheng, Mei Lu, Xinyuan Liu, Xiaoying Gong, Quanjun Fang, Zhiying Yin, Canjie Zheng
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
传播 仍然是中国的公共卫生重点,从指示病例到配偶的家庭内部 传播 有助于持续进行 传播。该研究旨在量化 HBV 血清流行率 并评估报告的 HBV 病例配偶之间的知识差距,为基于家庭的预防策略提供信息。
设计与对象
2024年,在衢州市随机选择的三个县开展了一项横断面调查,纳入了2023年1月至2024年9月期间报告的乙型肝炎病例的18岁及以上配偶。
核心方法
参与者完成了乙型肝炎知识的结构化调查问卷,并提供了血液样本,用于 HBsAg、抗 HBs、抗 HBe、抗 HBc 和 HBeAg 的血清学检测。多元逻辑回归确定了知识意识的相关性。
主要结果
344 名有效受访者中,161 名提供了血液样本;HBsAg 阳性率为 11.80%,抗-HBs 阳性率为 60.87%,抗-HBe 阳性率为 23.60%,抗-HBc 阳性率为 77.02%,没有参与者的 HBeAg 检测呈阳性。乙肝知识总体知晓率为36.69%,合格率为22.09%。
解释与创新
作者将高HBV感染率和低知识水平解读为家庭内传播风险显著,并强调需针对老年人、农民/工人及不了解自身感染状况者开展有针对性的健康教育。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持通过医疗机构和在线平台提供以家庭为中心的干预措施,包括健康教育、传播推广和定期检测,以减少家庭传播并改善乙肝控制。
局限与证据强度
横截面设计和对自我报告数据的依赖限制了因果推断,并可能低估 患病率;由于潜在的选择和信息偏差以及缺乏基因型或病毒载量数据,证据是中等的。

Hepatitis B seroprevalence and knowledge awareness among spouses of reported Hepatitis B cases in Quzhou city: A cross-sectional study

Authors: Wangfeng Zheng, Mei Lu, Xinyuan Liu, Xiaoying Gong, Quanjun Fang, Zhiying Yin, Canjie Zheng
Original abstract (English)
This study investigated Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection prevalence and disease-related knowledge among spouses of reported HBV cases in Quzhou City to support family-based interventions reducing intra-familial transmission. In 2024, a cross-sectional survey was conducted in three randomly selected counties. Spouses (≥18y) of cases reported from Jan 2023 to Sep 2024 were interviewed with a structured questionnaire and tested for serological markers. Among 344 valid respondents, 161 provided blood samples. The HBsAg positivity rate was 11.80%. The rates for anti-HBs, anti-HBe, and anti-HBc were 60.87%, 23.60%, and 77.02%, respectively. No participant tested positive for HBeAg. Based on serological patterns, 12.42% were classified as susceptible, 9.94% had immunity from vaccination, and 50.93% had evidence of past infection. Overall HBV knowledge awareness rate was 36.69% with a pass rate of 22.09%. The awareness rates for the 10 specific knowledge items ranged from 6.40% to 62.21%. Multivariate logistic regression analysis showed that older age (≥60y: OR = 0.102, 95%CI:0.040 - 0.261), farmers/workers(OR = 0.335, 95%CI:0.155 - 0.723), and unawareness of personal infection status(OR = 0.364, 95%CI:0.159 - 0.833) were independently associated with lower knowledge. Most respondents preferred obtaining information through "hospitals/medical staff"(61.63%) and "online platforms/WeChat official accounts"(38.95%). Spouses have high HBsAg prevalence and poor HBV knowledge, indicating a substantial risk of intra-family transmission. Targeted health education, especially for the elderly, farmers/workers, and those unaware of their status, should be delivered through medical institutions to promote vaccination and regular testing, thereby reducing family‐clustered transmission.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B remains a public-health priority in China, and intra-familial transmission from index cases to spouses contributes to ongoing transmission. The study aimed to quantify HBV seroprevalence and assess knowledge gaps among spouses of reported HBV cases to inform family-based prevention strategies.
Design and population
A cross-sectional survey was conducted in 2024 across three randomly selected counties in Quzhou City, enrolling spouses aged 18 years or older of HBV cases reported between January 2023 and September 2024.
Core methods
Participants completed a structured questionnaire on HBV knowledge and provided blood samples for serological testing of HBsAg, anti-HBs, anti-HBe, anti-HBc, and HBeAg. Multivariate logistic regression identified correlates of knowledge awareness.
Main results
Among 344 valid respondents, 161 provided blood samples; HBsAg positivity was 11.80%, anti-HBs 60.87%, anti-HBe 23.60%, anti-HBc 77.02%, and no participant tested positive for HBeAg. Overall HBV knowledge awareness was 36.69% with a pass rate of 22.09%.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the high HBsAg prevalence and low knowledge as indicating substantial intra-familial transmission risk, and they highlight the need for targeted education, especially for older adults, farmers/workers, and those unaware of their infection status.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support family-focused interventions—health education, vaccination promotion, and regular testing—delivered through medical institutions and online platforms to reduce household transmission and improve HBV control.
Limitations and evidence strength
The cross-sectional design and reliance on self-reported data limit causal inference and may underestimate prevalence; the evidence is moderate due to potential selection and information biases and the absence of genotype or viral load data.

乙型肝炎 疫苗 在泰国北部山区部落和无国籍儿童中诱导免疫力

Hepatitis B vaccine-induced immunity among hill tribe and stateless children in northern Thailand
作者： Tawatchai Apidechkul, Manassawin Kampun, Buathanya Srikua, Fartima Yeemard, Watchareewan Apidechkul, Chalitar Chomchoei, Panupong Upala, Ratipark Tamornpark
摘要中文翻译本研究的目的是确定泰国北部完全和部分接种疫苗的山地部落和五岁无国籍儿童之间血清保护的差异,以及自 1992 年开始实施 疫苗 以来这些儿童缺乏抗 HBs 的相关因素。进行了一项病例对照研究,收集居住在清莱府 Mae Fah Luang 区村庄的山地部落和无国籍个人的信息。未事先免疫、延迟免疫或不完全乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 的儿童被纳入其中。完全接种疫苗的儿童被纳入对照。发放调查问卷并采集3 mL血样,用于乙型肝炎生物标志物的定性和定量检测。应用逻辑回归来检测与抗-HBs缺乏相关的因素。研究共纳入188例病例和188例对照;54%为男性,54.3%属于阿卡族。抗-HBs总体患病率为59.3%;完全型疫苗接种患儿(其中2例抗-HBc阳性)中抗-HBs检出率为55.3%,不完全型疫苗接种患儿中检出率为55.3%。出生体重<2500 g(AOR = 3.52;95% CI = 1.39–9.12)、母亲属于瑶族(AOR = 16.90;95% CI = 2.00–142.57)、母亲信奉基督教(AOR = 2.30;95% CI = 1.18–4.46)、父亲高龄的儿童30-39 岁(AOR = 2.20;95% CI = 1.01-4.80)更有可能表现出抗-HBs 缺失。山区部落和无国籍儿童中乙型肝炎 疫苗 引起的 免疫应答 率仍然不理想,需要有效的评估计划来监测这一问题。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在确定泰国北部完全和部分接种疫苗的山地部落和五岁无国籍儿童之间血清保护的差异,以及自 1992 年开始实施 疫苗 以来这些儿童缺乏抗 HBs 的相关因素。
设计与对象
进行了一项病例对照研究,以收集居住在清莱府 Mae Fah Luang 区村庄的山地部落和无国籍人士的信息。未事先免疫、延迟免疫或不完全乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 的儿童被纳入其中。
核心方法
发放调查问卷,采集血样3 mL,用于乙型肝炎生物标志物的定性和定量检测。应用逻辑回归来检测与抗-HBs缺失相关的因素。
主要结果
抗-HBs总体患病率为59.3%;完全型疫苗接种患儿(其中2例抗-HBc阳性)中抗-HBs检出率为55.3%,不完全型疫苗接种患儿中检出率为55.3%。
解释与创新
该研究强调,山区部落和无国籍儿童中乙型肝炎 疫苗 引起的 免疫应答 率仍然不理想,这表明需要有效的评估计划来监测这一问题。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,监测易感人群中 疫苗 诱导的免疫力对于改进 疫苗接种 策略并确保充分预防乙型肝炎具有重要意义。
局限与证据强度
该研究包括 188 例病例和 188 名对照组,其中 54% 为男性,54.3% 属于阿卡组,但证据仅限于抽象数据,可能无法捕捉详细的临床或流行病学细微差别。

Hepatitis B vaccine-induced immunity among hill tribe and stateless children in northern Thailand

Authors: Tawatchai Apidechkul, Manassawin Kampun, Buathanya Srikua, Fartima Yeemard, Watchareewan Apidechkul, Chalitar Chomchoei, Panupong Upala, Ratipark Tamornpark
Original abstract (English)
The aim of this study was to determine the difference in seroprotection between fully and partially vaccinated hill tribe and stateless children aged five years in northern Thailand along with the factors associated with the absence of anti-HBs in these children since vaccine implementation began in 1992. A case–control study was conducted to collect information from hill tribe and stateless individuals living in villages in Mae Fah Luang District, Chiang Rai Province. Children with no prior immunization, delayed immunization, or incomplete hepatitis B vaccination were included. Children who were completely vaccinated were included as controls. A questionnaire was distributed and 3 mL blood samples were collected for the qualitative and quantitative detection of hepatitis B biomarkers. Logistic regression was applied to detect factors associated with the absence of anti-HBs. A total of 188 cases and 188 controls were included in the study; 54% were male, and 54.3% belonged to the Akha group. The overall prevalence of anti-HBs was 59.3%; 55.3% of anti-HBs was detected in children with complete vaccination (with 2 children positive for anti-HBc), and 55.3% was detected in children with incomplete vaccination. Children whose birth weight was <2500 g (AOR = 3.52; 95% CI = 1.39–9.12), whose mother belonged to the Yao group (AOR = 16.90; 95% CI = 2.00–142.57), whose mother practiced Christianity (AOR = 2.30; 95% CI = 1.18–4.46), and whose father was aged 30–39 years (AOR = 2.20; 95% CI = 1.01–4.80) were more likely to exhibit an absence of anti-HBs. The hepatitis B vaccine-induced immune response rate among hill tribe and stateless children remains undesirable, and an effective assessment program is needed to monitor this issue.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to determine the difference in seroprotection between fully and partially vaccinated hill tribe and stateless children aged five years in northern Thailand, as well as the factors associated with the absence of anti-HBs in these children since vaccine implementation began in 1992.
Design and population
A case–control study was conducted to collect information from hill tribe and stateless individuals living in villages in Mae Fah Luang District, Chiang Rai Province. Children with no prior immunization, delayed immunization, or incomplete hepatitis B vaccination were included.
Core methods
A questionnaire was distributed, and 3 mL blood samples were collected for the qualitative and quantitative detection of hepatitis B biomarkers. Logistic regression was applied to detect factors associated with the absence of anti-HBs.
Main results
The overall prevalence of anti-HBs was 59.3%; 55.3% of anti-HBs was detected in children with complete vaccination (with 2 children positive for anti-HBc), and 55.3% was detected in children with incomplete vaccination.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights that the hepatitis B vaccine-induced immune response rate among hill tribe and stateless children remains undesirable, indicating a need for effective assessment programs to monitor this issue.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest the importance of monitoring vaccine-induced immunity in vulnerable populations to improve vaccination strategies and ensure adequate protection against hepatitis B.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study included 188 cases and 188 controls, with 54% male and 54.3% belonging to the Akha group, but the evidence is limited to abstract-only data, which may not capture detailed clinical or epidemiological nuances.

2011 年至 2021 年英国丁型肝炎病毒的特征。

Characterising Hepatitis D Virus in the UK From 2011 to 2021.
作者： R Simmons, S Ijaz, R Jackson, K Jack, R Mulongeni, R Raghu, A Riddell, A Elsharkawy, A Uriel, A Koffas
摘要中文翻译在英国,人们对 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 的双重感染知之甚少。建议对所有 HBV 感染者进行 HDV 筛查,因为合并感染会导致疾病进展加快。利用英国 11 个报告进行 HDV 检测的实验室共享的 HDV 检测数据以及全国 HBV 检测数据,我们建立了 HDV 检测个体的 队列研究,调查检测途径,描述 HDV-RNA 阳性的特征和结果,为检测、诊断 和护理联系的针对性干预措施提供信息。我们收集了 HDV-RNA 阳性个体的人口统计信息、临床评估、治疗 和 NHS 信托机构的最新实验室结果,并通过与国家医疗保健数据集的链接来收集。2011 年至 2021 年间,英格兰新诊断出的 HBV 患者中有 42% 与 HDV 检测有关。抗 HDV 阳性率为 5.0%,55.8% 进行过 HDV-RNA 检测,45.7% 曾经呈 HDV-RNA 阳性。在没有抗 HDV 结果的情况下,HDV-RNA 阳性率为 6.4%。在 HDV-RNA 阳性的个体中,94.3% 为非英国出生,报告有 24 种不同的主要语言,49% 有 治疗 证据,48% 有肝硬化证据。10%的人死于肝病,其中58.4%死于肝病。总之,我们的研究结果表明,在英国,针对 HBV 阳性个体的 HDV 检测相对于检测指南(包括抗 HDV 阳性者的 RNA 检测)来说并不是最佳的。需要做大量的工作来改善患者护理途径,以确保患者得到适当的诊断、联系和保留护理,并有足够的机会获得 治疗。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了英国对 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 双重感染缺乏了解的问题,突显了 HBV 感染者在了解检测实践和临床结果方面的差距。
设计与对象
使用来自 11 个英国实验室的 HDV 检测数据和国家 HBV 检测数据进行了一项回顾性 队列研究 研究,以建立 2011 年至 2021 年间接受 HDV 检测的个体的 队列研究。
核心方法
该研究收集了来自 NHS 信托机构的人口统计信息、临床评估、治疗 记录和实验室结果以及 HDV-RNA 阳性个体的国家医疗保健数据集。
主要结果
在英格兰新诊断出 HBV 的个体中,42% 与 HDV 检测有关;抗 HDV 阳性率为 5.0%,55.8% 进行过 HDV-RNA 检测,45.7% 曾经呈 HDV-RNA 阳性。在 HDV-RNA 阳性个体中,94.3% 为非英国出生,49% 有 治疗 证据,48% 有肝硬化证据。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,与检测指南(包括抗 HDV 阳性病例中的 RNA 检测)相比,英国 HBV 阳性个体的 HDV 检测效果欠佳,这表明需要改进诊断途径。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要采取有针对性的干预措施来改善丁型肝炎病毒检测、诊断,以及与英国 HBV 感染者的护理联系起来,以减少疾病进展和 诊断。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于来自有限数量的实验室的回顾性数据,这些实验室可能无法捕获所有测试途径或结果,并且由于潜在的选择和报告偏差,证据强度中等。

Characterising Hepatitis D Virus in the UK From 2011 to 2021.

Authors: R Simmons, S Ijaz, R Jackson, K Jack, R Mulongeni, R Raghu, A Riddell, A Elsharkawy, A Uriel, A Koffas
Original abstract (English)
Little is known about hepatitis B virus (HBV) and Hepatitis D virus (HDV) coinfection in the UK. HDV screening is recommended among all individuals with HBV, with coinfection associated with increased disease progression. Using data on HDV testing, shared by 11 laboratories in the UK who reported undertaking HDV testing, and national testing data for HBV we established a cohort of individuals tested for HDV, investigating testing pathways, describing characteristics and outcomes of those HDV-RNA positive to inform targeted interventions for testing, diagnosis and linkage to care. Demographic information, clinical assessments, treatment and recent laboratory results from NHS trusts, and through linkage to national healthcare datasets was collected for HDV-RNA positive individuals. Between 2011 and 2021, 42% of individuals newly diagnosed with HBV in England were linked to an HDV test. Anti-HDV positivity was 5.0%, 55.8% were HDV-RNA tested and 45.7% were ever HDV-RNA positive. HDV-RNA positivity in the absence of an anti-HDV result was 6.4%. Among individuals who were HDV-RNA positive, 94.3% were non-UK born, with 24 different primary languages reported, 49% had evidence of treatment, and 48% had evidence of cirrhosis. 10% had died of which 58.4% died of liver disease. In conclusion, our findings indicate that HDV testing in HBV positive individuals in the UK is sub-optimal against testing guidelines, including RNA testing among those who are anti-HDV positive. Considerable work is needed to improve the patient care pathway, to ensure patients are appropriately diagnosed, linked and retained in care, with adequate access to treatment.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the lack of knowledge about hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis D virus (HDV) coinfection in the UK, highlighting a gap in understanding testing practices and clinical outcomes among HBV-infected individuals.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study was conducted using HDV testing data from 11 UK laboratories and national HBV testing data to establish a cohort of individuals tested for HDV between 2011 and 2021.
Core methods
The study collected demographic information, clinical assessments, treatment records, and laboratory results from NHS trusts and national healthcare datasets for HDV-RNA positive individuals.
Main results
Among individuals newly diagnosed with HBV in England, 42% were linked to an HDV test; anti-HDV positivity was 5.0%, 55.8% were HDV-RNA tested, and 45.7% were ever HDV-RNA positive. Among HDV-RNA positive individuals, 94.3% were non-UK born, 49% had evidence of treatment, and 48% had evidence of cirrhosis.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors conclude that HDV testing in HBV-positive individuals in the UK is sub-optimal compared to testing guidelines, including RNA testing among anti-HDV positive cases, indicating a need for improved diagnostic pathways.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for targeted interventions to improve HDV testing, diagnosis, and linkage to care among HBV-infected individuals in the UK to reduce disease progression and mortality.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on retrospective data from a limited number of laboratories, which may not capture all testing pathways or outcomes, and the evidence strength is moderate due to potential selection and reporting biases.

乌兹别克斯坦的乙型和丁型肝炎负担:对 血清流行率 的综合分析以及全国范围研究的风险相关性

Hepatitis B and D burden in Uzbekistan: comprehensive analysis of seroprevalence and risk correlates from a countrywide study
作者： Erkin Isakovich Musabaev, Malika Erkinovna Khodjaeva, Aziza Saydullaevna Khikmatullaeva, Nargiz Sapievna Ibadullaeva, Fatima Shokir kizi Shodieva, Gulnara Karimovna Khudaykulova, Nigora Ubaydullaevna Tadjieva, Sobirjon Bakhtiyorovich Amonov, Ulugbek Khudayberdievich Mirzaev, Фатима Шокир қизи Шодиева
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管自 1998 年以来乌兹别克斯坦在全国范围内实施了 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染,但乌兹别克斯坦仍然承受着巨大的 传播 (HBV) 感染负担,而且对疾病分布和 传播 动态的区域差异仍知之甚少。
设计与对象
2022 年 7 月至 2024 年 6 月在乌兹别克斯坦进行了一项全国性横断面研究,通过分层随机抽样从七个地区的 1,048,575 名个体的父母 队列研究 中筛选出 10,552 名成年人。
核心方法
血清学检测包括乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)、乙型肝炎核心抗原抗体(抗-HBc)和病毒RNA检测,并使用多变量逻辑回归来识别乙型肝炎病毒感染的独立危险因素。
主要结果
Overall HBsAg prevalence was 4.7% (95% CI: 4.3–5.1%), with geographic heterogeneity ranging from 3.2% in Tashkent to 6.3% in Bukhara; 68.1% of HBsAg-positive individuals had detectable HBV DNA, and 12.7% had HDV superinfection.
解释与创新
The study highlights persistent HBV burden with notable regional clustering and identifies distinct transmission pathways for HDV superinfection, including a specific association with sharing personal hygiene items (aOR 2.34, 95% CI: 1.16–4.74; P = 0.02).
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持对未接种疫苗的成年人加强筛查和 治疗 以及有针对性的家庭预防措施,特别是 HDV 传播,以推进乌兹别克斯坦消除肝炎的努力。
局限与证据强度
横断面设计限制了因果推断,并且依赖血清学标志物可能会低估隐匿性乙型肝炎病毒感染;然而,大量的地理分层样本和稳健的统计方法强化了证据。

Hepatitis B and D burden in Uzbekistan: comprehensive analysis of seroprevalence and risk correlates from a countrywide study

Authors: Erkin Isakovich Musabaev, Malika Erkinovna Khodjaeva, Aziza Saydullaevna Khikmatullaeva, Nargiz Sapievna Ibadullaeva, Fatima Shokir kizi Shodieva, Gulnara Karimovna Khudaykulova, Nigora Ubaydullaevna Tadjieva, Sobirjon Bakhtiyorovich Amonov, Ulugbek Khudayberdievich Mirzaev, Фатима Шокир қизи Шодиева
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Uzbekistan faces a substantial burden of hepatitis B and D virus (HBV and HDV) infections despite implementing universal hepatitis B vaccination in 1998. Regional variation in disease distribution and contemporary transmission dynamics remain inadequately characterized. Our objective was to evaluate the geographically stratified prevalence of HBV and HDV, while also identifying the key risk factors fueling transmission within the adult population of Uzbekistan. METHODS: We conducted a nationwide cross-sectional study of 10,552 adults across seven regions of Uzbekistan from July 2022 to June 2024. Participants were selected using stratified random sampling from a parent cohort of 1,048,575 individuals. Serological testing included hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg), antibodies to hepatitis B core antigen (anti-HBc), anti-HDV antibodies, and viral RNA detection. Multivariate logistic regression identified independent risk factors for HBV and HDV infections. RESULTS: Overall HBsAg prevalence was 4.7% (95% CI: 4.3-5.1%), with substantial geographic heterogeneity ranging from 3.2% in Tashkent to 6.3% in Bukhara. Among HBsAg-positive individuals, 68.1% had detectable HBV DNA and 12.7% had HDV superinfection. HDV RNA prevalence among HBV DNA-positive individuals ranged from 2.5% to 23.7% across regions. Age-cohort analysis demonstrated vaccination program impact, with older unvaccinated adults bearing disproportionate disease burden. Female sex associated with 31% lower odds of chronic HBsAg carriage. Married status, healthcare-associated exposures, and body piercings correlated with increased anti-HBc positivity. Remarkably, sharing personal hygiene items showed striking HDV-specific association (aOR 2.34, 95% CI: 1.16-4.74; P = 0.02) but no association with HBV markers, suggesting distinct transmission pathways for HDV superinfection. CONCLUSIONS: Persistent HBV burden with notable regional clustering and HDV superinfection necessitates targeted interventions. The identification of personal hygiene item sharing as a specific HDV transmission route provides an actionable household-level prevention target. Intensified screening and treatment of unvaccinated adults remain critical for hepatitis elimination efforts.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Uzbekistan continues to experience a significant burden of hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection despite nationwide vaccination since 1998, and regional variation in disease distribution and transmission dynamics remains poorly understood.
Design and population
A nationwide cross-sectional study was conducted in Uzbekistan from July 2022 to June 2024, enrolling 10,552 adults selected via stratified random sampling from a parent cohort of 1,048,575 individuals across seven regions.
Core methods
Serological testing included hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg), antibodies to hepatitis B core antigen (anti-HBc), and viral RNA detection, with multivariate logistic regression used to identify independent risk factors for HBV infection.
Main results
Overall HBsAg prevalence was 4.7% (95% CI: 4.3–5.1%), with geographic heterogeneity ranging from 3.2% in Tashkent to 6.3% in Bukhara; 68.1% of HBsAg-positive individuals had detectable HBV DNA, and 12.7% had HDV superinfection.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights persistent HBV burden with notable regional clustering and identifies distinct transmission pathways for HDV superinfection, including a specific association with sharing personal hygiene items (aOR 2.34, 95% CI: 1.16–4.74; P = 0.02).
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support intensified screening and treatment of unvaccinated adults and targeted household-level prevention measures, particularly for HDV transmission, to advance hepatitis elimination efforts in Uzbekistan.
Limitations and evidence strength
The cross-sectional design limits causal inference, and reliance on serological markers may underestimate occult HBV infection; however, the large, geographically stratified sample and robust statistical methods strengthen the evidence.

用于诊断中国乙型肝炎患者肝细胞癌的 GADA 模型的开发和验证。

Development and validation of GADA model for diagnosing hepatocellular carcinoma in Chinese hepatitis B patients.
作者： Mingjie Yao, Yamei Wei, Weilin Gu, Pengfei Wang, Zian Li, Dazhi Zhang, Hao Liao, Qingxian Cai, Fang Luo, Yong Xia
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染是全球肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的主要原因,但 HBV 相关 HCC 的诊断工具需要进一步优化。该研究旨在开发一种针对 HBV 相关 HCC 的诊断模型,以弥补当前诊断性能的差距。
设计与对象
使用 2190 名患有 HBV 相关肝病(包括 慢性乙型肝炎、肝硬化和 HCC)患者的数据开发并验证了诊断模型 (GADA)。对 10 家医院的 20,908 名患者进行了外部验证。
核心方法
GADA 模型是通过从 GALAD 模型中省略 AFP-L3 来创建的,以提高可访问性,并使用受试者工作特征曲线下面积 (AUC) 来评估其诊断性能。计算敏感性和特异性来评估模型性能。
主要结果
In the modeling group, the GADA model achieved an AUC of 0.940 (95% CI, 0.927–0.952) with sensitivity 82.57% and specificity 91.53%, outperforming the original GALAD model (AUC 0.907). For early-stage HCC, the AUC was 0.858 in the modeling group and 0.868 in the internal validation group. External validation across 20,908 participants yielded an AUC of 0.919 with sensitivity 86.00% and specificity 81.88%.
解释与创新
与 GALAD 模型相比,GADA 模型表现出卓越的诊断性能,特别是对于早期 HCC 检测。作者强调了它在提高 HBV 相关 HCC 诊断准确性和降低误诊率方面的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
GADA 模型可以改善 HBV 相关 HCC 的早期检测,从而实现及时干预和更好的患者预后。其在多个中心的可靠验证支持了 HBV 流行地区的潜在临床实用性。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于回顾性数据和基于医院的 队列研究,这可能会限制普遍性。虽然外部验证包括大量样本,但该模型在不同人群和现实环境中的表现需要进一步评估。

Development and validation of GADA model for diagnosing hepatocellular carcinoma in Chinese hepatitis B patients.

Authors: Mingjie Yao, Yamei Wei, Weilin Gu, Pengfei Wang, Zian Li, Dazhi Zhang, Hao Liao, Qingxian Cai, Fang Luo, Yong Xia
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection accounts for more than 40% of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) cases worldwide. The GALAD (Gender, Age, alpha fetoprotein heterogeneity [AFP-L3], AFP, and des-gamma-carboxy prothrombin [PIVKA-II/DCP]) model has potential for enhancing HCC diagnosis. However, its diagnostic performance in HBV-associated chronic liver disease requires further optimization. This study aimed to establish a diagnostic model for HBV-associated HCC. METHODS: The data from 2190 patients with HBV-related liver diseases, including chronic hepatitis B (CHB), cirrhosis, and HCC were analysed. The GADA model was developed by omitting AFP-L3 for improved accessibility, and its performance was assessed via the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC). Validation was performed on 20,908 patients across ten hospitals. RESULTS: The simplified GADA model demonstrated exceptional diagnostic performance in identifying HBV-related HCC in the modeling group, achieving an AUC of 0.940 (95% CI, 0.927-0.952), with a sensitivity and specificity of 82.57% and 91.53%, respectively, surpassing the original GALAD model's AUC of 0.907 (95% CI, 0.891-0.921). In the internal validation cohort, the GADA model had an AUC of 0.935 (95% CI, 0.914-0.953), sensitivity of 82.04%, and specificity of 92.65%. For early-stage HCC detection, the AUC in the modeling group was 0.858 (95% CI, 0.837-0.877), and in the internal validation group was 0.868 (95% CI, 0.836-0.895), significantly better than the original GALAD model. External validation with 20,908 participants yielded an AUC of 0.919 (95% CI, 0.916-0.923), with sensitivity of 86.00% and specificity 81.88%. For early-stage HCC diagnosis, the AUC was 0.903 (95% CI: 0.898-0.907), with sensitivity of 88.16% and specificity 74.50%. CONCLUSIONS: The GADA model shows exceptional promise for diagnosing HBV-related HCC, enhancing early detection and reducing misdiagnosis rates. Its clinical utility is supported by robust validation, indicating significant implications for patient prognosis and treatment options.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection is a major cause of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) worldwide, yet diagnostic tools for HBV-associated HCC require further optimization. The study aimed to develop a diagnostic model tailored for HBV-associated HCC to address gaps in current diagnostic performance.
Design and population
A diagnostic model (GADA) was developed and validated using data from 2190 patients with HBV-related liver diseases, including chronic hepatitis B, cirrhosis, and HCC. External validation was performed on 20,908 patients across ten hospitals.
Core methods
The GADA model was created by omitting AFP-L3 from the GALAD model to improve accessibility, and its diagnostic performance was evaluated using the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC). Sensitivity and specificity were calculated to assess model performance.
Main results
In the modeling group, the GADA model achieved an AUC of 0.940 (95% CI, 0.927–0.952) with sensitivity 82.57% and specificity 91.53%, outperforming the original GALAD model (AUC 0.907). For early-stage HCC, the AUC was 0.858 in the modeling group and 0.868 in the internal validation group. External validation across 20,908 participants yielded an AUC of 0.919 with sensitivity 86.00% and specificity 81.88%.
Interpretation and novelty
The GADA model demonstrated superior diagnostic performance compared to the GALAD model, particularly for early-stage HCC detection. The authors highlight its potential to enhance diagnostic accuracy and reduce misdiagnosis rates in HBV-associated HCC.
Scientific and public-health significance
The GADA model may improve early detection of HBV-related HCC, enabling timely intervention and better patient prognosis. Its robust validation across multiple centers supports potential clinical utility in HBV-endemic regions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on retrospective data and hospital-based cohorts, which may limit generalizability. While external validation included a large sample, the model’s performance in diverse populations and real-world settings requires further evaluation.
