A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

美国成人和高危人群对甲型和乙型肝炎的血清学免疫

Serologic Immunity to Hepatitis A and B in US Adults and High-Risk Populations
作者： Giovanni A. Roldan, Jesse Fletcher, Timothy Davie, Thomas M. Leventhal, John R. Lake, Prowpanga Udompap
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述检查了美国成年人中的人类 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染情况,重点关注血清学免疫 患病率 以及与 疫苗 衍生的 HBV 免疫相关的因素,特别针对 20 岁或以上的成年人以及慢性肝病、慢性肾病、糖尿病、免疫抑制和妊娠患者等高风险亚群 (A2、A5)。
证据基础与方法
该评价采用横断面设计,数据来自 2017 年 1 月至 2023 年 8 月的国家健康和营养检查调查 (NHANES),包括 13,514 名具有可用 HBV 血清学检测结果的成年人 (A3)。对2026年1月3日至5月10日的数据进行了分析,并进行加权以生成具有全国代表性的估计值(A4、A6)。多变量调查加权逻辑回归模型确定了与 HBV 免疫相关的独立因素 (A7)。
共识与结论
The review concludes that population-level serologic immunity to HBV was suboptimal, with 27.1% (95% CI, 25.8%-28.4%) demonstrating serologic immunity and 25.2% (95% CI, 23.9%-26.5%) exhibiting vaccine-derived immunity, corresponding to approximately 61.3 million and 56.9 million individuals, respectively (A9). Vaccine-derived HBV immunity was associated with younger age, female sex, non-Hispanic Asian and non-Hispanic Black race and ethnicity, and higher educational attainment (A12).
争议与缺口
The review highlights substantial susceptibility to HBV across high-risk clinical subgroups, with vaccine-derived immunity ranging from 14.0% (95% CI, 11.3%-16.6%) in chronic kidney disease to 38.6% (95% CI, 28.0%-49.1%) in pregnancy, indicating persistent gaps in protection despite vaccination efforts (A13).
科研与实践启示
该综述强调需要针对乙肝病毒(特别是高危人群)制定有针对性的、系统的 疫苗接种 策略,以解决免疫力不佳的问题并降低易感性 (A15)。

Serologic Immunity to Hepatitis A and B in US Adults and High-Risk Populations

Authors: Giovanni A. Roldan, Jesse Fletcher, Timothy Davie, Thomas M. Leventhal, John R. Lake, Prowpanga Udompap
Original abstract (English)
IMPORTANCE: Serologic immunity to hepatitis A virus (HAV) and hepatitis B virus (HBV) protects against acute infection and severe outcomes among high-risk groups; however, contemporary data on population-level immunity remain limited, especially in high-risk populations. OBJECTIVE: To estimate the prevalence and factors associated with serologic immunity in the overall population and across high-risk subgroups, including those with chronic liver disease (CLD), chronic kidney disease, diabetes, and immunosuppression and pregnant people. DESIGN, SETTING, AND PARTICIPANTS: This cross-sectional study used data from the National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey from January 2017 to August 2023 and included adults aged 20 years or older with available HAV and HBV serologic test results. Data were analyzed from January 3 to May 10, 2026. MAIN OUTCOMES AND MEASURES: The primary outcomes were the prevalence of serologic immunity to HAV, defined by hepatitis A antibody positivity, and HBV, defined by hepatitis B surface antibody positivity, with vaccine-derived immunity specifically identified by surface antibody positivity in the absence of hepatitis B core antibodies. Data were weighted to generate nationally representative estimates of the US adult population. Multivariable survey-weighted logistic regression models were used to identify independent factors associated with viral hepatitis immunity. RESULTS: Among 13 514 individuals, representing an estimated 226.1 million US adults (mean [SE] age, 48.65 [0.40] years; 51.76% female), 39.5% (95% CI, 37.7%-41.3%) demonstrated serologic immunity to hepatitis A, corresponding to approximately 89.4 million individuals. For HBV, 27.1% (95% CI, 25.8%-28.4%) demonstrated serologic immunity, corresponding to approximately 61.3 million individuals, while vaccine-derived immunity was present in 25.2% (95% CI, 23.9%-26.5%). In adjusted models, HAV immunity was associated with younger age (eg, 20-29 vs ≥65 years: adjusted odds ratio [AOR], 0.51; 95% CI, 0.44-0.60); lower educational attainment (eg, <grade 9 vs college graduate or above: AOR, 0.32; 95% CI, 0.24-0.42); non-Hispanic Black (AOR, 1.67; 95% CI, 1.34-2.09), Mexican American (AOR, 4.22; 95% CI, 3.42-5.21), other Hispanic (AOR, 2.01; 95% CI, 1.60-2.54), non-Hispanic Asian (AOR, 3.03; 95% CI, 2.45-3.76), and multiracial or other (AOR, 1.36; 95% CI, 1.04-1.79) race and ethnicity; being born outside the US (AOR, 4.54; 95% CI, 3.74-5.52); and liver disease awareness (OR, 1.65; 95% CI, 1.29-2.09). Obesity was associated with lower odds of HAV immunity (AOR, 0.83; 95% CI, 0.72-0.96). Vaccine-derived HBV immunity was associated with younger age (eg, 20-29 vs ≥65 years: AOR, 0.18; 95% CI, 0.15-0.23), female sex (AOR, 1.36; 95% CI, 1.18-1.56), non-Hispanic Asian (AOR, 1.47; 95% CI, 1.17-1.86) and non-Hispanic Black (AOR, 1.15; 95% CI, 1.00-1.32) race and ethnicity, and higher educational attainment (eg, <grade 9 vs college graduate or above: AOR, 3.29; 95% CI, 2.35-4.61). In high-risk subgroups, HAV immunity ranged from 26.7% (95% CI, 18.9%-34.4%) for metabolic dysfunction-associated alcohol-related liver disease to 63.7% (95% CI, 50.0%-77.4%) for chronic HBV infection, while HBV vaccine-derived immunity ranged from 14.0% (95% CI, 11.3%-16.6%) for chronic kidney disease to 38.6% (95% CI, 28.0%-49.1%) for pregnancy. CONCLUSIONS AND RELEVANCE: This cross-sectional study found that population-level serologic immunity to HAV and HBV was suboptimal and substantial susceptibility persisted across high-risk clinical subgroups. These findings highlight persistent gaps in viral hepatitis protection and support targeted, systematic vaccination strategies for HAV and HBV, especially among high-risk populations.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines human hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection in US adults, focusing on serologic immunity prevalence and factors associated with vaccine-derived HBV immunity, specifically targeting adults aged 20 years or older and high-risk subgroups such as those with chronic liver disease, chronic kidney disease, diabetes, immunosuppression, and pregnancy (A2, A5).
Evidence base and method
The review uses a cross-sectional design with data from the National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey (NHANES) from January 2017 to August 2023, including 13,514 adults with available HBV serologic test results (A3). Data were analyzed from January 3 to May 10, 2026, and weighted to generate nationally representative estimates (A4, A6). Multivariable survey-weighted logistic regression models identified independent factors associated with HBV immunity (A7).
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that population-level serologic immunity to HBV was suboptimal, with 27.1% (95% CI, 25.8%-28.4%) demonstrating serologic immunity and 25.2% (95% CI, 23.9%-26.5%) exhibiting vaccine-derived immunity, corresponding to approximately 61.3 million and 56.9 million individuals, respectively (A9). Vaccine-derived HBV immunity was associated with younger age, female sex, non-Hispanic Asian and non-Hispanic Black race and ethnicity, and higher educational attainment (A12).
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights substantial susceptibility to HBV across high-risk clinical subgroups, with vaccine-derived immunity ranging from 14.0% (95% CI, 11.3%-16.6%) in chronic kidney disease to 38.6% (95% CI, 28.0%-49.1%) in pregnancy, indicating persistent gaps in protection despite vaccination efforts (A13).
Research and practice implications
The review emphasizes the need for targeted, systematic vaccination strategies for HBV, particularly among high-risk populations, to address suboptimal immunity and reduce susceptibility (A15).

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

吉尔吉斯斯坦共和国成年人对乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎的认识、知识和实践——2024 年全国代表性调查的结果

Hepatitis B and hepatitis C awareness, knowledge, and practices among adults in the Kyrgyz Republic—results from a nationally representative survey, 2024
作者： Su N. Aung, Aibek A. Bekbolotov, Nazira Usmanova, Venera Maitieva, Umutkhan Chokmorova, Aigul Solpueva, Mirbek Tursunbekov, Gulnara Esenkeldieva, Shaun Shadaker, Chynarkul Zhumalieva
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎是肝硬化的主要驱动因素,肝硬化是吉尔吉斯共和国 (KR) 的主要死亡原因。评估乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎的认识、知识和行为实践对于指导预防、检测和教育干预非常重要。方法 2024 年 6 月至 9 月期间,我们采用分层多阶段整群抽样的方式对韩国 6090 名成年人进行了横断面调查。我们通过面对面访谈进行了问卷调查,以评估乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎的认识、知识、实践(测试和 疫苗接种)以及健康信息来源。在了解乙型肝炎和/或丙型肝炎的受访者中,我们评估了总体知识和与低知识相关的因素(定义为乙型肝炎正确回答的问题≤11/17,丙型肝炎正确回答的问题≤12/18,每个正确回答得分为1)。我们进行了多变量回归,以确定与乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎意识和知识相关的社会人口因素和实践。结果 在 6090 名目标成年人中,有 5243 人完成了访谈。总体而言,43.7% 的人听说过乙型肝炎,29.2% 听说过丙型肝炎。总体而言,分别有 28.6% 和 25.4% 的人曾接受过乙型肝炎或丙型肝炎检测,11.6% 的人接种过乙型肝炎疫苗。在了解乙型肝炎或丙型肝炎的参与者中,24.6% 和 28.2% 正确回答了所有 传播 问题,19.4% 和 19.4% 的人正确回答了所有 传播 问题。分别有 4.2% 的人正确回答了所有预防问题。女性、与中等教育相比的高等教育以及之前的丙型肝炎检测均与乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎意识及相关知识呈正相关。与乙型肝炎知识得分低相关的因素包括男性、年龄 50-59 岁(相比之下,年龄为 18-29 岁)以及家庭主妇。与丙型肝炎知识较低相关的因素包括男性、家庭主妇以及之前没有进行过丙型肝炎检测(P 值 < 0.05)。互联网是最常见的可信健康信息来源(87.4%)。结论 该研究表明需要提高乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎的认识和知识水平,以及检测和 Z​​XQTERMLQXZ 的吸收水平。为了在韩国消除乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎,需要制定针对特定社会人口群体的教育计划。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述调查了吉尔吉斯共和国成人中的人类 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染,重点关注作为 HBV 相关肝硬化和肝细胞癌驱动因素的认识、知识和实践(测试和 疫苗接种)。核心问题涉及乙型肝炎的认识和知识水平以及影响这些结果的相关社会人口因素。
证据基础与方法
该审查报告了 2024 年 6 月至 9 月针对吉尔吉斯共和国 6090 名成年人进行的分层多阶段整群抽样的横断面调查。面对面的调查问卷评估了乙肝病毒的认知、知识、检测、疫苗接种 和健康信息来源。多变量回归确定了与乙型肝炎意识和知识相关的社会人口因素和实践。
共识与结论
吉尔吉斯斯坦共和国成年人对乙肝病毒的认识(43.7%)和知识水平较低。只有 28.6% 的人曾经接受过 HBV 检测,11.6% 的人接种过 HBV 疫苗。女性、高等教育和既往 HCV 检测与 HBV 认知和知识呈正相关,而男性、年龄 50-59 岁以及家庭主妇与较低的 HBV 知识得分相关。
争议与缺口
该审查突显了人们对 HBV 认识 (43.7%) 和知识的显着差距,只有 19.4% 的人正确回答了所有 HBV 预防问题。互联网是最常见的可信健康信息来源 (87.4%),但其在提高 HBV 特异性知识方面的作用仍不清楚。该研究并未涉及 HBV 基因型或病毒学特征,限制了对 传播 动态或疾病严重程度的了解。
科研与实践启示
该审查强调需要开展有针对性的教育计划,以提高乙肝病毒的认识和知识,特别是男性、老年人(50-59 岁)和家庭主妇。它还强调了增加 HBV 检测和 Z​​XQTERMLQXZ 吸收的重要性,基于互联网的干预措施可能会利用对在线健康信息来源的高度依赖。

Hepatitis B and hepatitis C awareness, knowledge, and practices among adults in the Kyrgyz Republic—results from a nationally representative survey, 2024

Authors: Su N. Aung, Aibek A. Bekbolotov, Nazira Usmanova, Venera Maitieva, Umutkhan Chokmorova, Aigul Solpueva, Mirbek Tursunbekov, Gulnara Esenkeldieva, Shaun Shadaker, Chynarkul Zhumalieva
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Hepatitis B and hepatitis C are primary drivers of cirrhosis, a leading cause of death in the Kyrgyz Republic (KR). Assessing hepatitis B and hepatitis C awareness, knowledge, and behavioral practices are important to guide prevention, testing, and education interventions. Methods During June–September 2024, we conducted a cross-sectional survey using a stratified multi-stage cluster sampling targeting 6090 adults in KR. We administered a questionnaire through face-to-face interviews to assess hepatitis B and hepatitis C awareness, knowledge, practices (testing and vaccination), and sources of health information. Among respondents aware of hepatitis B and/or hepatitis C, we assessed overall knowledge and factors associated with low knowledge (defined as ≤ 11/17 questions answered correctly for hepatitis B and ≤ 12/18 questions answered correctly for hepatitis C, with each correct response scored as 1). We performed multivariable regression to identify sociodemographic factors and practices associated with hepatitis B and hepatitis C awareness and knowledge. Results Of 6090 targeted adults, 5243 completed the interview. Overall, 43.7% had heard of hepatitis B and 29.2% had heard of hepatitis C. Overall, 28.6% and 25.4% had ever been tested for hepatitis B or hepatitis C, respectively, and 11.6% had been vaccinated against hepatitis B. Among participants aware of hepatitis B or hepatitis C, 24.6% and 28.2% correctly answered all transmission questions, and 19.4% and 4.2% correctly answered all prevention questions, respectively. Female sex, post-secondary education compared to secondary education, and prior hepatitis C testing were all positively associated with hepatitis B and hepatitis C awareness and related knowledge. Factors associated with low knowledge score for hepatitis B included male sex, age 50–59 years (compared to 18–29 years), and being a homemaker. Factors associated with low hepatitis C knowledge were male sex, being a homemaker, and no prior hepatitis C testing ( P -value < 0.05). Internet was the most common source of trusted health information (87.4%). Conclusions The study demonstrated the need to improve levels of hepatitis B and hepatitis C awareness and knowledge, and testing and vaccination uptake. Education programs tailored to reach specific sociodemographic groups are needed to achieve hepatitis B and hepatitis C elimination in KR.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines human hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection in adults in the Kyrgyz Republic, focusing on awareness, knowledge, and practices (testing and vaccination) as drivers of HBV-related cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma. The central question addresses the levels of HBV awareness and knowledge and associated sociodemographic factors influencing these outcomes.
Evidence base and method
The review reports a cross-sectional survey conducted from June to September 2024 using stratified multi-stage cluster sampling targeting 6090 adults in the Kyrgyz Republic. A face-to-face questionnaire assessed HBV awareness, knowledge, testing, vaccination, and sources of health information. Multivariable regression identified sociodemographic factors and practices associated with HBV awareness and knowledge.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that HBV awareness (43.7%) and knowledge levels are low among adults in the Kyrgyz Republic. Only 28.6% had ever been tested for HBV, and 11.6% had been vaccinated against HBV. Female sex, post-secondary education, and prior HCV testing were positively associated with HBV awareness and knowledge, while male sex, age 50–59 years, and being a homemaker were associated with lower HBV knowledge scores.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights significant gaps in HBV awareness (43.7%) and knowledge, with only 19.4% correctly answering all HBV prevention questions. Internet was the most common source of trusted health information (87.4%), but its role in improving HBV-specific knowledge remains unclear. The study does not address HBV genotypes or virological characteristics, limiting insights into transmission dynamics or disease severity.
Research and practice implications
The review emphasizes the need for targeted education programs to improve HBV awareness and knowledge, particularly among males, older adults (50–59 years), and homemakers. It also underscores the importance of increasing HBV testing and vaccination uptake, with internet-based interventions potentially leveraging the high reliance on online health information sources.

乙型肝炎病毒 感染:病毒生物学、临床谱和预防策略的综合回顾

Hepatitis B Virus Infection: a Comprehensive Review of Viral Biology, Clinical Spectrum, and Preventive Strategies
作者： K. Kalybekova, R. Robin
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染是世界各地的一个严重的健康问题,也是慢性肝病、肝硬化和肝癌等长期肝脏问题的最常见原因之一。HBV 是由 乙型肝炎病毒 引起的,乙型肝炎病毒 是嗜肝DNA病毒科病毒家族的一部分,由部分双链 DNA 基因组 组成。传播 主要通过血液和体液的交换发生。本研究的目的是收集有关 HBV 感染的现有信息,并评估以下领域的信息:HBV 感染的 流行病学、病毒生物学、HBV 感染的临床特征、HBV 感染的 诊断 和 治疗 以及 HBV 感染的预防。本研究是基于从世界卫生组织 (WHO)、疾病控制与预防中心 (CDC) 获得的二手研究数据和同行评审文献的描述性文献综述。使用定性分析过程来提取已发表文献中包含的相关信息。乙型肝炎病毒感染有多种不同类型。有些人是无症状乙肝病毒携带者;患有急性乙型肝炎病毒感染的人;慢性乙型肝炎病毒感染者;以及患有由 HBV 引起的肝硬化和/或肝癌的个体。HBV 感染的 诊断 由与感染相关的血清学标志物的存在确定,例如 HBsAg、HBeAg、抗 HBc、抗 HBs 和 HBV DNA 水平。这些标记还有助于确定个体是否具有传染性以及疾病的进展程度。可用于治疗 HBV 感染的 抗病毒药物 疗法包括替诺福韦和恩替卡韦,它们已被证明可以有效抑制病毒复制,但无法消除病毒的共价闭合环状 (ccc) DNA。疫苗接种 针对 HBV 已证明事件病例减少,并且仍然是最有效的预防手段。作为可预防的全球健康问题,乙型肝炎病毒感染的负担仍在继续。正确诊断 HBV 感染者、用 抗病毒药物 药物治疗个体以及提供通用 疫苗接种 对于降低与 HBV 感染相关的发病率和 死亡率 是必要的。

Hepatitis B Virus Infection: a Comprehensive Review of Viral Biology, Clinical Spectrum, and Preventive Strategies

Authors: K. Kalybekova, R. Robin
Original abstract (English)
Infection with hepatitis B virus (HBV) is a serious health issue around the world, and one of the most common causes of long-term liver problems such as chronic liver disease and cirrhosis, and liver cancer. HBV is caused by the hepatitis B virus, which is part of the family of viruses called Hepadnaviridae and consists of a partially double-stranded DNA genome. Transmission occurs primarily through the exchange of blood and body fluids. The objective of this study was to collect available information about HBV infections and assess that information with respect to the following areas: epidemiology of HBV infection, viral biology, clinical features of HBV infection, diagnosis and treatment of HBV infection, and prevention of HBV infection. This study is a descriptive literature review based on the secondary research data acquired from the World Health Organization (WHO), the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC), and peer-reviewed literature. A qualitative analysis process was used to extract the relevant information contained in the published literature. There are many different types of HBV infections. There are individuals who are asymptomatic carriers of HBV; individuals who have an acute HBV infection; individuals with chronic HBV infection; and individuals with cirrhosis and/or liver cancer caused by HBV. The diagnosis of HBV infection is determined by the presence of serological markers associated with the infection, such as HBsAg, HBeAg, anti-HBc, anti-HBs, and HBV DNA levels. These markers also assist in determining whether or not the individual is infectious and how far progressed the disease is. Antiviral therapies available for treating HBV infection include tenofovir and entecavir which have been shown to effectively suppress viral replication but are unable to eliminate the covalently closed circular (ccc) DNA of the virus. Vaccination against HBV has demonstrated a reduction in the incident cases and remains the most effective means of prevention. The burden of HBV infection as a preventable global health issue continues. Properly diagnosing someone infected with HBV, treating the individual with an antiviral agent, and providing universal vaccination will be necessary to decrease the morbidity and mortality associated with HBV infection.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Infection with hepatitis B virus (HBV) is a serious health issue around the world, and one of the most common causes of long-term liver problems such as chronic liver disease and cirrhosis, and liver cancer.
Evidence base and method
HBV is caused by the hepatitis B virus, which is part of the family of viruses called Hepadnaviridae and consists of a partially double-stranded DNA genome. A qualitative analysis process was used to extract the relevant information contained in the published literature.
Consensus and conclusions
Vaccination against HBV has demonstrated a reduction in the incident cases and remains the most effective means of prevention.
Controversies and gaps
The burden of HBV infection as a preventable global health issue continues.
Research and practice implications
Properly diagnosing someone infected with HBV, treating the individual with an antiviral agent, and providing universal vaccination will be necessary to decrease the morbidity and mortality associated with HBV infection.

慢性乙型肝炎 与代谢功能障碍相关的脂肪肝疾病的“病毒-代谢”相互作用机制和管理挑战:从临床证据到综合多学科框架

The “viral–metabolic” interaction mechanisms and management challenges in chronic hepatitis B combined with metabolic dysfunction-associated fatty liver disease: from clinical evidence to an integrated multidisciplinary framework

The “viral–metabolic” interaction mechanisms and management challenges in chronic hepatitis B combined with metabolic dysfunction-associated fatty liver disease: from clinical evidence to an integrated multidisciplinary framework

Authors: Qianqian Zhu, Chengde Su, Qiaoqiao Yao, Ting Yang, Yali Xu, Mingdan Li, Qian Liu, Yawen Luo, Ping Yang
Original abstract (English)
The comorbidity of chronic hepatitis B and metabolic dysfunction-associated fatty liver disease involves a bidirectional “virus–metabolism” interaction mechanism, presenting a clinical paradox characterized by improved virological markers alongside an increased risk of hepatocellular carcinoma. Therefore, this article aims to systematically review the interaction mechanisms and regulatory factors between the two conditions, and to propose an integrated “virus–metabolism” dual-axis management framework that includes metabolism-stratified antiviral therapy and synchronous metabolic intervention, so as to provide a theoretical basis for stratified management and behavioral intervention of the comorbidity.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Therefore, this article aims to systematically review the interaction mechanisms and regulatory factors between the two conditions, and to propose an integrated “virus–metabolism” dual-axis management framework that includes metabolism-stratified antiviral therapy and synchronous metabolic intervention, so as to provide a theoretical basis for stratified management and behavioral intervention of the comorbidity.
Evidence base and method
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the review method, databases, eligibility process, or evidence base.
Consensus and conclusions
The comorbidity of chronic hepatitis B and metabolic dysfunction-associated fatty liver disease involves a bidirectional “virus–metabolism” interaction mechanism, presenting a clinical paradox characterized by improved virological markers alongside an increased risk of hepatocellular carcinoma.
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not clearly state controversies or evidence gaps.
Research and practice implications
The supplied evidence does not clearly state a specific research or practice implication.

血清流行率 的 乙型肝炎病毒 9 月 28 日前往几内亚科纳克里 Dixinn 市体育场医疗健康中心的患者的感染和血液学特征:一项横断面研究

Seroprevalence of Hepatitis B Virus Infection and Hematological Profile of Patients Attending the 28th September Stadium Medical Health Center, Dixinn Municipality, Conakry, Guinea: A Cross-Sectional Study
作者： Authors unavailable
摘要中文翻译IJGHC - 国际绿色和草药化学杂志|开放获取的同行评审研究期刊,发表绿色化学、草药化学和可持续化学领域的高质量研究。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该研究调查了 9 月 28 日在几内亚科纳克里 Dixinn 市体育场医疗健康中心就诊的患者中 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染的 血清流行率 及其与血液学特征的关联。核心问题是确定该人群中乙型肝炎病毒感染的程度及其对血液学参数的影响。
证据基础与方法
该研究是在单个医疗中心进行的横断面分析。它不涉及系统评价或荟萃分析,而是侧重于从就诊患者那里收集的数据。该方法包括乙型肝炎病毒感染的血清学检测和参与者的血液学分析。
共识与结论
该研究报告了人群中 HBV 感染的 血清流行率 并将其与特定的血液学结果联系起来。然而,由于研究范围有限,没有提供汇总估计或共识结论。
争议与缺口
该研究并未解决与乙型肝炎病毒感染相关的争议或证据差距。它缺乏更广泛的人群数据,也没有探索观察到的血液学关联的机制或含义。
科研与实践启示
研究结果表明需要对当地人群的乙型肝炎病毒感染进行有针对性的筛查和监测。然而,除了这一一般性建议之外,该研究并未提供具体的研究或实践意义。

Seroprevalence of Hepatitis B Virus Infection and Hematological Profile of Patients Attending the 28th September Stadium Medical Health Center, Dixinn Municipality, Conakry, Guinea: A Cross-Sectional Study

Scope and question
The study investigates the seroprevalence of hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection and its association with hematological profiles among patients attending the 28th September Stadium Medical Health Center in Dixinn Municipality, Conakry, Guinea. The central question is to determine the extent of HBV infection in this population and its impact on hematological parameters.
Evidence base and method
The study is a cross-sectional analysis conducted at a single medical center. It does not involve a systematic review or meta-analysis, but rather focuses on data collected from patients attending the facility. The methodology includes serological testing for HBV infection and hematological profiling of the participants.
Consensus and conclusions
The study reports the seroprevalence of HBV infection in the population and links it to specific hematological findings. However, no pooled estimates or consensus conclusions are provided due to the limited scope of the study.
Controversies and gaps
The study does not address controversies or evidence gaps related to HBV infection. It lacks broader population data and does not explore the mechanisms or implications of the observed hematological associations.
Research and practice implications
The findings suggest the need for targeted screening and monitoring of HBV infection in the local population. However, the study does not provide specific research or practice implications beyond this general suggestion.

慢性丁型肝炎感染的外周生物标志物:综述

Peripheral Biomarkers in Chronic Hepatitis D Infection: A Review
作者： Zillah Cargill, James Lok, Geoffrey Dusheiko, Ivana Carey
摘要中文翻译摘要 慢性丁型肝炎是最严重的病毒性肝炎,与乙型肝炎单一感染相比,晚期纤维化、肝硬化和肝细胞癌的发生率更高。它是由丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 引起的,这是一种有缺陷的 RNA 病毒,需要与乙型肝炎 (HBV) 共同感染才能进入肝细胞并传播。使用当前可用的血清学生物标志物准确预测肝病后遗症和 治疗 反应仍然具有挑战性。本文综述了 HDV 的外周生物标志物,包括 HBV 活性的新型生物标志物,探讨了其潜在的临床用途和未来发展的领域。外周生物标志物能够对 HDV 患者进行风险分层、监测或预测 治疗 反应并阐明感染的自然史(包括与 HBV 的复杂关系),将能够实现个体化护理并早期识别那些罹患晚期肝病风险较高的患者。在现有的 治疗 和新兴的 抗病毒药物 疗法的背景下,确定这些生物标志物在更大的、种族多样化的人群中的作用将是未来的当务之急。

Peripheral Biomarkers in Chronic Hepatitis D Infection: A Review

Authors: Zillah Cargill, James Lok, Geoffrey Dusheiko, Ivana Carey
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Chronic hepatitis D represents the most severe form of viral hepatitis and is associated with higher rates of advanced fibrosis, cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma than hepatitis B monoinfection. It is caused by the hepatitis D virus (HDV), a defective RNA virus that requires co‐infection with hepatitis B (HBV) for hepatocyte entry and propagation. Accurate prediction of liver disease sequelae and treatment response using currently available serological biomarkers remains challenging. This article reviews peripheral biomarkers in HDV, including novel biomarkers of HBV activity, exploring their potential clinical utility and areas for future development. Peripheral biomarkers capable of risk stratifying patients with HDV, monitoring or predicting treatment response and elucidating the natural history of infection (including the complex relationship with HBV), would enable individualised care and early identification of those at greater risk of developing advanced liver disease. Establishing the role of these biomarkers in larger, ethnically diverse populations both on existing treatments and in the context of emerging antiviral therapies will be imperative going forward.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Chronic hepatitis D represents the most severe form of viral hepatitis and is associated with higher rates of advanced fibrosis, cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma than hepatitis B monoinfection.
Evidence base and method
It is caused by the hepatitis D virus (HDV), a defective RNA virus that requires co‐infection with hepatitis B (HBV) for hepatocyte entry and propagation.
Consensus and conclusions
Accurate prediction of liver disease sequelae and treatment response using currently available serological biomarkers remains challenging.
Controversies and gaps
Establishing the role of these biomarkers in larger, ethnically diverse populations both on existing treatments and in the context of emerging antiviral therapies will be imperative going forward.
Research and practice implications
Peripheral biomarkers capable of risk stratifying patients with HDV, monitoring or predicting treatment response and elucidating the natural history of infection (including the complex relationship with HBV), would enable individualised care and early identification of those at greater risk of developing advanced liver disease.

乙型肝炎无细胞 DNA 在无创产前检测中作为病毒感染性的早期生物标志物

Hepatitis B cell-free DNA in non-invasive prenatal testing as an early biomarker of viral infectivity
作者： V. N. Dao, Phuc Tri Nguyen, Thang Nhat Tran, N. H. Nguyễn, Hung‐Sang Tang, Maciej F. Boni, Hoa Giang, Duy Minh Phan, Nghia Hoai Nguyen, Dao, V. N.
摘要中文翻译无创产前检测 (NIPT) 最初是为了通过分析母体血液中的胎儿游离 DNA 来检测胎儿的染色体异常。最近的进展扩大了 NIPT 的应用范围,包括妊娠期间病毒感染的检测。然而,解释病原体来源的游离 DNA (cf-DNA) 在临床上仍然很复杂。本研究使用约 500,000 次 NIPT 就诊的数据集和 582 名孕妇(40 名 HBV 感染者)的独立验证 队列研究 来探讨 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) cf-DNA 的临床相关性,从人群和个人角度与 HBV 流行病学 进行对齐。我们的分析表明,HBV cf-DNA 是高病毒感染性的强生物标志物,而不是一般感染标志物,这表明它有可能在妊娠头三个月结束时识别出垂直 传播 风险较高的孕妇。此外,在妊娠第 9 - 17 周内,与 HBV 阴性女性相比,HBV 阳性女性的胎儿分数出现小幅但持续的下降,这与 HBV 对胎盘无细胞 DNA 贡献的早期影响相一致,尽管观察性设计和未测量的母体协变量排除了因果推断。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
无创产前检测 (NIPT) 最初是为了通过分析母体血液中的胎儿游离 DNA 来检测胎儿的染色体异常。最近的进展扩大了 NIPT 的应用范围,包括妊娠期间病毒感染的检测。
证据基础与方法
本研究使用约 500,000 次 NIPT 就诊的数据集和 582 名孕妇(40 名 HBV 感染者)的独立验证 队列研究 来探讨 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) cf-DNA 的临床相关性,从人群和个人角度与 HBV 流行病学 进行对齐。
共识与结论
我们的分析表明,HBV cf-DNA 是高病毒感染性的强生物标志物,而不是一般感染标志物,这表明它有可能在妊娠头三个月结束时识别出垂直 传播 风险较高的孕妇。
争议与缺口
此外,在妊娠第 9 - 17 周内,与 HBV 阴性女性相比,HBV 阳性女性的胎儿分数出现小幅但持续的下降,这与 HBV 对胎盘无细胞 DNA 贡献的早期影响相一致,尽管观察性设计和未测量的母体协变量排除了因果推断。
科研与实践启示
该研究表明,HBV cf-DNA 可用于在妊娠前三个月结束时识别垂直 传播 风险较高的孕妇,这可能为有针对性的干预措施和 监测 策略提供信息。

Hepatitis B cell-free DNA in non-invasive prenatal testing as an early biomarker of viral infectivity

Authors: V. N. Dao, Phuc Tri Nguyen, Thang Nhat Tran, N. H. Nguyễn, Hung‐Sang Tang, Maciej F. Boni, Hoa Giang, Duy Minh Phan, Nghia Hoai Nguyen, Dao, V. N.
Original abstract (English)
Non-invasive prenatal testing (NIPT) was initially developed to detect chromosomal abnormalities in fetuses through the analysis of cell-free fetal DNA in maternal blood. Recent advancements have expanded NIPT's applications to include the detection of viral infections during pregnancy. However, interpreting pathogen-derived cell-free DNA (cf-DNA) remains clinically complex. This study explores the clinical relevance of hepatitis B virus (HBV) cf-DNA using a dataset of approximately 500,000 NIPT visits and an independent validation cohort of 582 pregnant women (40 HBV-infected), aligned with HBV epidemiology from both population and individual perspectives. Our analysis reveals that HBV cf-DNA is a strong biomarker of high viral infectivity rather than a general marker of infection, suggesting its potential to identify pregnant women at heightened risk of vertical transmission by the end of the first trimester. Additionally, HBV-positive women showed a small but consistent reduction in fetal fraction relative to HBV-negative women across gestational weeks 9 - 17, an association compatible with an early effect of HBV on the placental contribution to cell-free DNA, although the observational design and unmeasured maternal covariates preclude causal inference.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Non-invasive prenatal testing (NIPT) was initially developed to detect chromosomal abnormalities in fetuses through the analysis of cell-free fetal DNA in maternal blood. Recent advancements have expanded NIPT's applications to include the detection of viral infections during pregnancy.
Evidence base and method
This study explores the clinical relevance of hepatitis B virus (HBV) cf-DNA using a dataset of approximately 500,000 NIPT visits and an independent validation cohort of 582 pregnant women (40 HBV-infected), aligned with HBV epidemiology from both population and individual perspectives.
Consensus and conclusions
Our analysis reveals that HBV cf-DNA is a strong biomarker of high viral infectivity rather than a general marker of infection, suggesting its potential to identify pregnant women at heightened risk of vertical transmission by the end of the first trimester.
Controversies and gaps
Additionally, HBV-positive women showed a small but consistent reduction in fetal fraction relative to HBV-negative women across gestational weeks 9 - 17, an association compatible with an early effect of HBV on the placental contribution to cell-free DNA, although the observational design and unmeasured maternal covariates preclude causal inference.
Research and practice implications
The study suggests that HBV cf-DNA could be used to identify pregnant women at heightened risk of vertical transmission by the end of the first trimester, which may inform targeted interventions and surveillance strategies.

急性乙型肝炎血清学恢复期间钩端螺旋体病的合并感染:病例报告 - Cureus

Co-infection With Leptospirosis During Acute Hepatitis B Serologic Recovery: A Case Report - Cureus
新闻简报病例报告 描述了一名 30 岁的女性,她最初出现急性乙型肝炎症状,包括肌痛、黄疸和转氨酶升高。她接受了替诺福韦艾拉酚胺治疗,但病情没有改善。进一步评估显示钩端螺旋体 IgM 血清学 呈阳性,使用改良费恩标准得出钩端螺旋体病 诊断。该病例凸显了诊断钩端螺旋体病和病毒性肝炎的挑战,因为其症状与急性乙型肝炎非常相似。该患者接受了美罗培南治疗,临床和生化指标迅速改善。该病例强调了将钩端螺旋体病视为急性肝损伤的潜在隐藏原因的重要性,特别是在该疾病流行的地区。患者两周后出院,并计划两周后进行随访。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该病例报告于2026年提交,患者症状始于入院前10天,并在使用抗钩端螺旋体病药物治疗后两天内病情好转。该报告的发表日期为2026-08-19。
地点与对象
该病例涉及一名 30 岁女性,地点不明,可能是钩端螺旋体病流行的地区,例如热带或亚热带地区。受影响的人群是患有急性乙型肝炎并可能同时感染钩端螺旋体病的个体。
事件
一名30岁女性最初被诊断患有急性乙型肝炎,但后来发现合并感染钩端螺旋体病,通过钩端螺旋体IgM 血清学并修改Faine的标准进行鉴定。该病例凸显了区分这两种情况的诊断挑战。
规模、影响与风险
该病例涉及一名急性乙型肝炎患者并同时感染钩端螺旋体病。该患者出现了严重的高胆红素血症和凝血功能障碍,这对于解决乙型肝炎而言是非典型的。钩端螺旋体病作为急性肝损伤的隐藏原因的风险得到了强调,特别是在流行地区。
应对、状态与不确定性
该患者接受美罗培南治疗,临床和生化指标迅速改善。诊断 使用修改后的 Faine 标准进行了确认。然而,该病例强调需要进一步调查和广泛区分 诊断 来治疗急性肝损伤,特别是在钩端螺旋体病流行的地区。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Co-infection With Leptospirosis During Acute Hepatitis B Serologic Recovery: A Case Report - Cureus

News Brief
This case report describes a 30-year-old woman who initially presented with symptoms of acute hepatitis B, including myalgia, jaundice, and elevated aminotransferases. She was treated with tenofovir alafenamide, but her condition did not improve. Further evaluation revealed positive Leptospira IgM serology, leading to a diagnosis of leptospirosis using modified Faine's criteria. The case highlights the challenges of diagnosing leptospirosis alongside viral hepatitis, as the symptoms can closely mimic those of acute hepatitis B. The patient was treated with meropenem, resulting in rapid clinical and biochemical improvement. This case underscores the importance of considering leptospirosis as a potential hidden cause of acute liver injury, particularly in regions where the disease is endemic. The patient was discharged after two weeks and scheduled for a follow-up two weeks later.
Original source text (English)
Abstract Leptospirosis is a zoonotic infection that can cause acute liver injury and mimic viral hepatitis. We report a 30-year-old woman presenting with myalgia, progressive jaundice, and markedly elevated aminotransferases, initially diagnosed with acute hepatitis B. She reported regular contact with her dog. Despite serologic recovery with hepatitis B surface antigen seroconversion, severe hyperbilirubinemia and coagulopathy were atypical for resolving hepatitis B. Further evaluation revealed positive Leptospira immunoglobulin M (IgM) serology, and leptospirosis was diagnosed using modified Faine's criteria. Treatment with meropenem resulted in rapid clinical and biochemical improvement, highlighting leptospirosis as an important hidden cause of acute liver injury. Introduction Leptospirosis is a globally important zoonotic infection caused by pathogenic Leptospira species, with the greatest burden occurring in tropical and subtropical regions because the climate and poor hygienic conditions favor the pathogen's survival and distribution, particularly in Southeast Asia [1]. Reported disease incidence ranged from 0.10 to 975 annual cases per 100,000 population. The annual morbidity and mortality due to leptospirosis worldwide were estimated to be 14.77 cases per 100,000 population and 0.84 deaths per 100,000 population, respectively [1]. Rodents, especially rats, are the most important reservoirs. Leptospirosis can be transmitted through direct contact with urine, blood, or tissue from an infected animal or more commonly through exposure to contaminated environments. Because leptospires can survive in a humid environment for months, water is an important vehicle for transmission [2]. Acute liver injury is a common clinical presentation in gastroenterology. Leptospirosis is an underrecognized cause of acute liver injury and can closely mimic acute viral hepatitis with tender hepatomegaly, jaundice, and elevated aminotransferases [2]. The clinical spectrum ranges from a mild, self-limited febrile illness to a severe, life-threatening condition, with a mortality rate approaching to 10% in severe cases [3]. Diagnostic challenges arise when leptospirosis presents concurrently with viral hepatitis, as serologic findings may be misleading. Spontaneous seroconversion during acute hepatitis B may falsely reassure clinicians, despite ongoing liver injury from an alternative etiology. We report a case of leptospirosis presenting during the serologic recovery phase of acute hepatitis B, resulting in a diagnostic delay. This case highlights the importance of maintaining a broad differential diagnosis for acute liver injury and recognizing leptospirosis as a potential hidden cause, especially in regions where the disease is endemic. Case Presentation A 30-year-old woman was admitted with a 10-day history of myalgia, predominantly involving the back and legs, and nausea. Over the following five days, she developed chills, progressive jaundice, and scleral icterus, along with dark yellow urine, without abdominal pain. At that time, she was diagnosed with acute hepatitis B and was treated with tenofovir alafenamide 25 mg daily for five days at a previous hospital; however, her condition failed to improve. She had no known past medical history. She was not taking any medications, herbal products, or dietary supplements and had not received the hepatitis B vaccine. There was no family history of viral hepatitis. The patient reported regular contact with her dogs and no exposure to contaminated water. She was not a smoker. The hematologic and biochemical results are shown in Table 1 and Table 2, indicating changes over different time points. Urinalysis showed no proteinuria or hematuria, with negative nitrites and leukocytes. Abdominal ultrasound revealed decreased hepatic echogenicity without other abnormalities. Therefore, abdominal magnetic resonance imaging was performed to rule out biliary obstruction. While magnetic resonance cholangiopancreatography showed a nondilated common bile duct, abdominal magnetic resonance imaging demonstrated periportal edema (Figure 1). Chest radiography revealed no abnormalities. The patient was diagnosed with leptospirosis and additionally treated with meropenem 1 g three times daily for 14 days, along with fluid supplementation and vitamin K. Tenofovir alafenamide was stopped after the diagnosis of leptospirosis. Marked clinical improvement was observed within two days. She was discharged after two weeks and scheduled for a follow-up two weeks later. Discussion This case highlights an unusual presentation of leptospirosis manifesting as acute liver injury. Hepatic involvement in leptospirosis can closely mimic acute viral hepatitis, presenting with jaundice, cholestasis, and elevated aminotransferases, as observed in our patient [2]. Acute hepatitis B is a self-limited infectious disease caused by hepatitis B virus, with spontaneous recovery occurring in more than 95% of immunocompetent patients with acute hepatitis B. Patients can present with fatigue, anorexia, nausea, jaundice, and markedly elevated aminotransferase levels. The diagnosis of acute hepatitis B is established by the presence of hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) for less than six months or by the presence of immunoglobulin M (IgM) hepatitis B core antibody (anti-HBc) and hepatitis B surface antibody (anti-HBs) in the absence of HBsAg [4]. Leptospirosis is a globally important zoonotic infection caused by pathogenic Leptospira species, occurring predominantly in tropical and subtropical regions. Transmission occurs through direct contact with infected animal body fluids or exposure to contaminated water or soil. During the initial incubation period, leptospires can be isolated from the bloodstream. Leptospira can survive in the host by evading parts of the innate immune response such as complement-mediated killing and phagocytosis. Earlier studies have demonstrated an association between an exaggerated proinflammatory immune response and mortality. During the immune phase, the appearance of antibodies coincides with the disappearance of leptospires from the blood. Nevertheless, the bacteria can persist in various organs, including the liver, lungs, heart, brain, and kidneys [3]. Clinical severity ranges from mild illness characterized by fever, nausea, and vomiting to severe leptospirosis (Weil's syndrome), characterized by jaundice, hemorrhage, and acute kidney injury, with jaundice resulting primarily from cholestasis rather than hepatocellular necrosis [3,5]. Liver histopathology may demonstrate focal necrosis rather than widespread hepatocellular necrosis, foci of inflammation, and plugging of bile canaliculi. Experimental work showed the infiltration of Leptospira into the Disse space and migration between hepatocytes with detachment of the intercellular and disruption of bile canaliculi leading to bile leakage [3]. Because both diseases share overlapping clinical manifestations, distinguishing leptospirosis from acute hepatitis B based on clinical and biochemical findings can be challenging, particularly in atypical cases and endemic areas where both infections can coexist. Diagnosis of leptospirosis remains challenging due to nonspecific clinical features and limitations of diagnostic testing. Faine's criteria and modified Faine's criteria, which integrate clinical, epidemiologic, and serologic findings, provide useful diagnostic tools, particularly in resource-limited settings [6,7]. Serologic tests may be negative early in the disease course, as antibodies typically develop after five days, whereas molecular techniques allow earlier detection but are not universally available [8]. Vietnam is located in a region with high leptospirosis endemicity; however, the disease remains underdiagnosed due to overlapping clinical features with other causes of acute liver injury and limited access to diagnostic testing [9]. In our patient, serologic testing demonstrated seroconversion from HBsAg to anti-HBs and the presence of IgM anti-HBc, suggesting recovery from acute hepatitis B [10]. Nevertheless, the persistence of severe hyperbilirubinemia and coagulopathy was atypical for resolving acute hepatitis B and prompted further evaluation. Subsequent testing revealed positive Leptospira IgM serology, supporting the diagnosis of leptospirosis based on modified Faine's criteria, with a total score of 29 points. Even though our case did not meet the classic criteria for Weil's syndrome, persistent hyperbilirubinemia and coagulation abnormalities were risk factors for severe disease in acute hepatitis. Despite common renal involvement in leptospirosis, its absence does not exclude the diagnosis [3]. Penicillin and doxycycline are first-line therapies for leptospirosis; meropenem has been reported to be effective in severe disease, especially when first-line antibiotics are unavailable or contraindicated [11,12]. Our patient showed rapid clinical and biochemical improvement following treatment with meropenem, which supports its potential role in severe leptospirosis. Moreover, the rapid clinical and biochemical response to antimicrobial therapy provided additional supportive evidence for the diagnosis of leptospirosis. However, the absence of confirmatory molecular testing or a microscopic agglutination test remained a limitation of our case. To our knowledge, only a few cases of leptospirosis and hepatitis B co-infection have been reported. Previous reports described patients with Weil's syndrome and concurrent hepatitis B, whereas the nature of the hepatitis B virus infection was not clearly established [13,14]. In contrast, our patient had serologically confirmed acute hepatitis B in the recovery phase, while persistent biochemical abnormalities prompted further investigation and ultimately led to the diagnosis of leptospirosis, highlighting the importance of considering concurrent infections when the clinical course is atypical. Conclusions This case highlights leptospirosis as an important differential diagnosis of acute liver injury, even in the absence of renal involvement. In patients with acute hepatitis B, persistent or disproportionate biochemical abnormalities despite serologic recovery should prompt evaluation for alternative or concurrent etiologies. Early recognition of leptospirosis and appropriate antimicrobial therapy may lead to rapid clinical improvement and favorable outcomes. However, the absence of confirmatory molecular testing or microscopic agglutination test limits the certainty of the causal relationship between meropenem treatment and clinical improvement. A silent virus: Why Alberta doctors want more people tested for hepatitis B - ctvnews.ca
新闻简报A recent news report highlights the growing concern among Alberta doctors about the silent nature of hepatitis B virus (HBV) and the need for increased testing. A national survey commissioned by GSK Canada found that nearly nine in 10 Canadians have little or no awareness of chronic hepatitis B, with only 14 per cent having ever been tested. The survey also revealed misconceptions, such as the belief that there is no treatment for chronic HBV and that vaccination is unnecessary. In Alberta, an estimated 34,000 people are affected by the virus, and over 95 per cent of newly reported chronic cases in 2024 were acquired outside Canada. The provincial government is focusing testing efforts on high-risk groups, including pregnant women and those with known risk factors, and is collaborating with community partners to improve access to testing and treatment. The report emphasizes the importance of early detection and awareness to prevent the progression of the disease, which can lead to severe liver conditions like cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma. The article underscores the challenges posed by the virus's asymptomatic progression and the need for better public education.

Live tariff updates here.Dismiss breaking news alert ADVERTISEMENT Share current article via EmailShare current article via XShare current article via RedditShare current article via LinkedIn Calgary News A silent virus: Why Alberta doctors want more people tested for hepatitis B By Mark Villani Opens in new window Published: August 18, 2026 at 4:31p.m. EDT Albertans are being encouraged to get tested for hepatitis B after a new survey found nearly nine in 10 Canadians have little awareness of the virus. Two years ago, Jeff Lumsdon walked into a Calgary plasma donation clinic expecting to help someone else. Instead, he left with a diagnosis that would change his own life. After routine blood testing, the 45-year-old received a phone call asking him to come back for additional screening. The information you need to know, sent directly to you: Download the CTV News App The follow-up tests confirmed he had chronic hepatitis B — a virus he had never suspected he was carrying. “I went through the five stages of grief really quickly,” Lumsdon said. “I would be lying if I told you I wasn’t immediately depressed, upset, angry, shocked.” Like many Canadians, Lumsdon knew little about hepatitis B before his diagnosis. That lack of awareness is precisely what liver specialists say is allowing thousands of Albertans to live with the virus undetected. A new national survey commissioned and funded by GSK Canada found nearly nine in 10 Canadians have little or no awareness of chronic hepatitis B, while just 14 per cent say they have ever been tested. The virus affects more than 262,000 people nationwide – including an estimated 34,000 in Alberta – and is linked to 56 per cent of liver cancer cases globally. A disease with serious consequences Hepatitis B is a viral infection that attacks the liver. While some people clear the virus naturally, others develop chronic hepatitis B, which is a lifelong infection that can quietly damage the liver for decades before symptoms appear. “It’s the main cause of end-stage liver disease, or cirrhosis, and liver cancer worldwide,” said Dr. Carla Coffin, a hepatologist and professor of medicine at the University of Calgary’s Cumming School of Medicine. That silent progression is one of the disease’s greatest challenges. “Hepatitis B is asymptomatic, so people can live with it for their entire life without any symptoms until they develop end-stage liver disease,” Coffin said. Hepatitis B graphic Hepatitis B virus in the liver is depicted in this graphic. (CTV Your Morning Ottawa) Because many people never feel sick, they often don’t seek testing. By the time liver damage is discovered, treatment becomes more complicated. The GSK survey found misconceptions extend beyond testing. More than half of Canadians incorrectly believe there is no treatment for chronic hepatitis B, while roughly three-quarters are unaware that vaccination can prevent infection. An accidental diagnosis For Lumsdon, the diagnosis raised more questions than answers. “The very first thing that went through my mind is, ‘How long do I have to live?’” he said. “That was a question that went through my head for probably months because I didn’t know anything about it.” Doctors eventually determined he had chronic hepatitis B and started him on medication to suppress the virus. Today, he undergoes regular monitoring and says his outlook has changed dramatically. “We’ve developed a means to kind of treat it – or at least have some regular medicine that I take here that helps to maintain the current levels that I have,” he said. “Things have turned to a more positive light.” Lumsdon still however doesn’t know exactly when he contracted the virus. What he did discover was that he had never received a hepatitis B vaccine as a child. Born in Saskatchewan in 1981, he was just old enough to miss widespread childhood immunization programs that were introduced in the year following his birth. “My parents thought I was fully vaccinated across the board, but I wasn’t – and it turns out that I never did get the vaccination for hepatitis.” Why Alberta’s screening approach matters Unlike some infectious diseases that are screened routinely, Alberta uses a risk-based approach for hepatitis B. The provincial government says testing is focused on people most likely to benefit, including pregnant women and those with known risk factors. “These screening pathways help health-care providers identify, test and support people living with hepatitis B,” the Ministry of Primary and Preventative Health Services said in a statement. The ministry also noted that more than 95 per cent of newly reported chronic hepatitis B cases in Alberta in 2024 were acquired outside Canada, and said it continues working with community partners to reduce barriers to testing and treatment. Coffin agrees Alberta has made significant progress in prevention. Since 2018, every infant born in the province has been offered hepatitis B vaccination, providing lifelong protection in most cases. “Infants at two months of age get the vaccine and then they’re protected for the rest of their life,” she said. But adults born before those programs — particularly newcomers from countries where hepatitis B is more common — may still be living with the virus unknowingly. “So, we have a pathway, it’s called the Alberta Primary Care Pathway that guides family doctors and guides patients about who should be tested for hepatitis B,” Coffin added. “The screening is currently just risk based – so, for example, if you think you were exposed to somebody with hepatitis B or born in a country with more prevalent risks to contracting hepatitis B – but you can also just ask for the test, talk to your primary care or family doctor about it and there will be no questions asked.” A growing push for broader screening The Canadian Association for the Study of the Liver now recommends universal hepatitis B screening for adults; a shift Coffin says reflects growing recognition that risk-based testing misses too many people. “Early detection can help prevent serious liver damage,” she said. While there is currently no cure for chronic hepatitis B, antiviral medications can suppress the virus and reduce the risk of liver disease progression. “We have very good, effective antiviral drugs that will reduce the risk of liver disease progression,” Coffin said. “You can live a long and normal life.” For Lumsdon, his diagnosis also exposed how little many people understand about the virus. “There’s a lot of misinformation and a lot of just lack of information regarding the disease,” he said. “I’ve had to take it upon myself to explain about a lot of it to people.” He says that lack of awareness is exactly why more people need to talk about hepatitis B and consider getting tested. “I would implore anybody — whether there’s uncertainty, whether you have this fear that maybe you’ve contracted it — you should get tested,” Lumsdon said. And while his diagnosis was difficult, he now sees knowing as an opportunity to take control of his health. “I’m happy that I know now because I know what I’m looking for,” he said. “If there’s a means for you to get treatment, to get help, to speak with doctors and be able to prolong your life, I would say that is far more beneficial than living without knowing that you have it.” Report an Error Editorial standards & policies Why you can trust CTV News Top Stories Brooks RCMP release new photos of suspect’s truck in connection with incendiary devices investigation August 20, 2026 at 3:43p.m. EDT Playground testers wanted: 100 kids sought to try out new structure August 20, 2026 at 3:20p.m. EDT Calgary mayor says brief shutdown of water main coming in September August 20, 2026 at 2:57p.m. EDT Friday flapjack breakfasts on schedule as Whoop-Up Days winds down in Lethbridge August 20, 2026 at 2:39p.m. EDT Calgary’s police chief talks guns, drugs and organized crime in a growing city August 20, 2026 at 1:54p.m. EDT New Calgary program teaches Grade 9 students about civic responsibly August 20, 2026 at 1:19p.m. EDT Editor's Picks Meet Natalie Harp, the aide so close to Trump she once rode in an SUV trunk to accompany him Prince Harry, Meghan’s reported return to U.K. a ‘significant’ move: royal commentator Police locate 3rd set of human remains on Smiths Falls, Ont. area property In Pictures Here's a look at all the wild food at the CNE this year Justin and Katy are only the latest: these celebrities have dated politicians or royalty This nearly $17-million cottage country estate was designed as a 'four-season residence' Toronto Provinces agree to put U.S. booze back on the shelves as trade minister returns to D.C. B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

监测乙型肝炎的疾病进展和肝癌风险 - 美国管理护理杂志

Monitoring Disease Progression and Liver Cancer Risk in Hepatitis B - The American Journal of Managed Care
新闻简报《美国管理护理杂志》的文章“监测乙型肝炎的疾病进展和肝癌风险”强调了为解决乙型肝炎相关健康挑战所做的持续努力。报告指出,资源匮乏地区的重新参与工作并未得到充分报告,强调需要改善准入和护理。这篇文章还讨论了影响 MASLD 管理的生活方式因素、Tobevibart 治疗丁型肝炎的疗效以及 JAK 抑制剂重塑白癜风 治疗 的潜力。提到了 B-ALL 和风险定制护理的研究,以及 acalabrutinib-venetoclax 组合改善 CLL 患者生活质量和安全性的研究结果。此外,本文还涉及高危 MM 的生存差距以及早期数据在评估 INCA033989 等治疗 CALR 突变骨髓纤维化的疗法中的作用。它还解决了更广泛的问题,例如 疫苗 安全、获得生殖保健以及社会决定因素对健康结果的影响,包括与种族主义和虐待相关的黑人女性心力衰竭风险较高。这些发现强调了有针对性的干预措施和进一步研究在管理乙型肝炎及其相关并发症方面的重要性。

时间
3天前、8天前、21天前、约1个月前、约1个月前、约1个月前、约1个月前、10天前、12天前、15天前。
地点与对象
资源匮乏的环境、未指定的地点、一般人群。
事件
资源匮乏地区的乙型肝炎重新参与工作报告不足;生活方式因素为 MASLD 管理提供了可行的目标;Tobevibart 组合对丁型肝炎的疗效;以纤维化为重点的终点可以重塑 MASH 试验设计;B-ALL 数据加强了对风险定制护理的推动;isatuximab OBI 可减少输注反应;acalabrutinib-venetoclax 组合可改善不同年龄的生活质量和安全性;tec-dara 缩小了高危 MM 的生存差距;早期数据支持 INCA033989 在 CALR 突变型骨髓纤维化中的作用;INCA033989可能解决突变型钙网蛋白骨髓纤维化中未满足的需求;定量 HBsAg 检测和有限疗程治疗;监测乙型肝炎的疾病进展和肝癌风险;为 RA 治疗 测序设定明确的目标并采取行动;视网膜血管疾病的获取和证据差距;JAK抑制剂重塑白癜风治疗目标;当前白癜风治疗景观;疫苗 安全栏更高;药剂师通过扩大覆盖范围获得报销;选择不接种疫苗的现实;疫苗 豁免滥用导致儿童免疫接种缺口;药房提供药物流产护理;atacicept 良好的安全性;夏季炎热加剧了微塑料暴露;转移性癌症推动医疗补助支出增加;重新考虑 CAR T 在 ALL 中的使用;药剂师扩大生殖保健服务的覆盖范围。
规模、影响与风险
资源匮乏地区的报告不足;与 死亡率 和心血管事件的较高风险相关;黑人女性心力衰竭的风险较高;突变型钙网蛋白骨髓纤维化的需求未得到满足;高风险 MM 的生存差距;更高的医疗补助支出;生殖保健服务获取方面的差距;视网膜血管疾病的证据差距。
应对、状态与不确定性
持续努力监测疾病进展和肝癌风险;研究针对风险的护理和 治疗 测序;探索新疗法及其安全性;对准入和报销问题的持续调查;乙型肝炎相关健康差异调查不足的领域。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Monitoring Disease Progression and Liver Cancer Risk in Hepatitis B - The American Journal of Managed Care

News Brief
The article 'Monitoring Disease Progression and Liver Cancer Risk in Hepatitis B' from The American Journal of Managed Care highlights ongoing efforts to address hepatitis B-related health challenges. It notes that re-engagement efforts in lower-resource settings are underreported, emphasizing the need for improved access and care. The piece also discusses lifestyle factors influencing MASLD management, the efficacy of Tobevibart in treating hepatitis D, and the potential of JAK inhibitors in reshaping vitiligo treatment. Research on B-ALL and risk-tailored care is mentioned, along with findings on acalabrutinib-venetoclax combos improving quality of life and safety in CLL patients. Additionally, the article touches on the survival gap in high-risk MM and the role of early data in evaluating therapies like INCA033989 for CALR-mutant myelofibrosis. It also addresses broader issues such as vaccine safety, access to reproductive health care, and the impact of social determinants on health outcomes, including higher heart failure risks in Black women linked to racism and abuse. These findings underscore the importance of targeted interventions and further research in managing hepatitis B and its associated complications.

监测乙型肝炎的疾病进展和肝癌风险

Monitoring Disease Progression and Liver Cancer Risk in Hepatitis B
新闻简报Bing News RSS 于 2026 年 8 月 20 日发表的文章“监测乙型肝炎的疾病进展和肝癌风险”讨论了监测 慢性乙型肝炎 的独特挑战。丙型肝炎通常会经历可预测的纤维化阶段,而乙型肝炎则不同,乙型肝炎可以导致肝癌,而不会因基因组整合而造成严重的肝损伤。专家 Raymond Chung 医学博士和 Douglas Dieterich 医学博士解释说,这种整合可以刺激癌基因并抑制肿瘤抑制基因,从而增加癌症风险。本文概述了监测策略,包括每年一次的纤维化弹性成像以及每两年一次使用 AFP 和超声进行的肝细胞癌筛查。对于患有晚期疾病、显着纤维化或特定危险因素(例如患有肝癌的一级亲属或基础核心启动子 突变)的患者,建议进行更频繁的筛查。重点是调整监测实践,以解决乙型肝炎的异质性进展及其相关癌症风险,而不报告确诊的 暴发 或病例。
查看新闻五要素
时间
广告视频播放器正在加载。
地点与对象
患有 慢性乙型肝炎 的一般人群,特别是患有晚期疾病、显着纤维化或特定危险因素(例如患有肝癌的一级亲属或基础核心启动子 突变)的人群。
事件
正如专家 Raymond Chung 医学博士和 Douglas Dieterich 医学博士在题为“监测乙型肝炎的疾病进展和肝癌风险”的文章中所讨论的那样,在 慢性乙型肝炎 中监测疾病进展和肝癌风险。该活动被描述为对基因组整合如何驱动肝癌风险以及监测应如何适应的回顾。
规模、影响与风险
文章强调,与丙型肝炎相比,乙型肝炎更令人担忧,因为该病毒直接整合到肝细胞 基因组 中,可能刺激癌基因并抑制肿瘤抑制基因。它提到每年两次使用 AFP 和超声进行肝细胞癌筛查,并对患有晚期疾病或特定危险因素的患者进行更频繁的筛查。
应对、状态与不确定性
本文讨论了监测策略,包括每年一次的纤维化弹性成像和每两年一次的肝细胞癌筛查。它不报告任何确诊的 暴发、病例或死亡,而是侧重于临床实践和风险因素。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Monitoring Disease Progression and Liver Cancer Risk in Hepatitis B

News Brief
The article 'Monitoring Disease Progression and Liver Cancer Risk in Hepatitis B' published on August 20, 2026, by Bing News RSS, discusses the unique challenges of monitoring chronic hepatitis B. Unlike hepatitis C, which typically progresses through predictable fibrosis stages, hepatitis B can lead to liver cancer without significant liver damage due to genomic integration. Experts Raymond Chung, MD, and Douglas Dieterich, MD, explain that this integration can stimulate oncogenes and suppress tumor-suppressor genes, increasing cancer risk. The article outlines monitoring strategies, including yearly elastography for fibrosis and biannual hepatocellular carcinoma screening using AFP and ultrasound. For patients with advanced disease, significant fibrosis, or specific risk factors such as a first-degree relative with liver cancer or a basal core promoter mutation, more frequent screening is recommended. The focus is on adapting monitoring practices to address the heterogeneous progression of hepatitis B and its associated cancer risk, without reporting confirmed outbreaks or cases.

Play Mute Current Time 0:00 / Duration 0:00 Loaded: 0% Stream Type LIVE Seek to live, currently playing liveLIVE Remaining Time -0:00 1x Playback Rate Picture-in-PictureFullscreen Episodes in this series Previous slideNext slide EP. 1 Understanding the Immune Pathophysiology Driving Chronic Hepatitis B EP. 2 Hepatitis B Epidemiology: Closing the Screening and Diagnosis Gap Now Playing EP. 3 Monitoring Disease Progression and Liver Cancer Risk in Hepatitis B EP. 4 Quantitative HBsAg Testing and the Path to Finite-Duration Therapy In "Monitoring Disease Progression and Liver Cancer Risk in Hepatitis B," our experts turn to how chronic hepatitis B silently raises liver cancer risk even without classic fibrosis. Dr. Chung notes that liver disease progression in chronic hepatitis B is often heterogeneous and asks Dr. Dieterich to describe the stages of progression and how he monitors for them in practice. Dr. Dieterich contrasts hepatitis B with hepatitis C, where fibrosis progresses fairly predictably over years due to ongoing inflammation. In hepatitis B, immune suppression driven by surface antigen excess often limits liver inflammation, so ALT and AST levels are frequently normal and only elevated in a small percentage of patients. As a result, fibrosis progression is not the primary concern. Liver cancer is the greater worry, he explains, because hepatitis B, unlike hepatitis C, integrates directly into the hepatocyte genome. That integration can stimulate oncogenes and suppress tumor-suppressor genes, raising cancer risk even in patients without meaningful fibrosis. His practice performs yearly elastography to check for fibrosis and biannual hepatocellular carcinoma screening with AFP and ultrasound, escalating to more frequent screening for patients with advanced disease, significant fibrosis, or specific risk factors such as a first-degree relative with liver cancer or a basal core promoter mutation. He describes one young patient with both a basal core promoter mutation and a brother diagnosed with liver cancer, for whom his team alternates MRI and ultrasound every year. He adds that suppressing the virus to an undetectable level, which is achievable with current tools, remains a central goal regardless of a patient's fibrosis stage. Dr. Chung closes by underscoring that because the disease can change unpredictably, longitudinal monitoring is essential at every stage, not just in patients who appear more advanced. In "Quantitative HBsAg Testing and the Path to Finite-Duration Therapy," the panel will unpack how quantitative testing is reshaping the treatment conversation. 新闻简报澳大利亚生物技术公司 Biotron 正准备将其乙型肝炎候选药物推进正式的临床前开发。此前新的实验室研究表明,抗病毒药物 对乙型肝炎 (HBV) 和丁型肝炎 (HDV) 病毒均具有强大的活性,包括与现有的针对丁型肝炎的 治疗 结合使用。BiotechDispatch 于 2026 年 8 月 18 日报道了这一进展。研究结果基于实验结果,不包括确诊病例、死亡或明确的风险陈述。该公司此举表明,该公司正在努力开发乙肝相关疾病的治疗方法。证据中没有提供有关影响规模或受影响的特定人群的进一步细节。这一发展是针对病毒感染的生物技术创新更广泛趋势的一部分,其他公司也在各自的治疗领域取得了长足的进步。

时间
The payment, worth approximately A$14.1 million, is the first instalment under an agreement that gives Everest exclusive rights to develop and commercialise DMX-200 across Greater China, South Korea and selected Southeast Asian markets.
地点与对象
比奥特隆,澳大利亚;人类 乙型肝炎病毒 感染。
事件
Biotron 正准备将其乙型肝炎候选药物推进正式的临床前开发,因为新的实验室研究发现该候选药物对乙型肝炎和丁型肝炎病毒表现出强大的 抗病毒药物 活性,包括与现有的针对丁型肝炎的 治疗 组合。这是 BiotechDispatch 报道的。
规模、影响与风险
所提供的证据中未报告。
应对、状态与不确定性
Biotron 基于有希望的实验室结果,正在将其乙型肝炎候选药物推进临床前开发。调查正在进行中,未提供明确的风险声明。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Biotron advances hepatitis B candidate towards human trials after promising HBV and HDV results - BiotechDispatch

News Brief
Biotron, an Australian biotechnology company, is preparing to advance its hepatitis B drug candidate into formal preclinical development. This follows new laboratory studies that demonstrated strong antiviral activity against both hepatitis B (HBV) and hepatitis D (HDV) viruses, including when combined with an existing treatment for hepatitis delta. The progress is reported by BiotechDispatch on August 18, 2026. The findings are based on experimental results and do not include confirmed cases, deaths, or explicit risk statements. The company's move indicates ongoing research efforts to develop therapies for HBV-related conditions. No further details on the scale of impact or specific population affected are provided in the evidence. The development is part of a broader trend in biotech innovation targeting viral infections, with other companies also making strides in their respective therapeutic areas.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

加纳的乙型肝炎:潜伏在阴影中的无声流行病 - 现代加纳

Hepatitis B in Ghana: A Silent Epidemic Lurking in the Shadows - Modern Ghana
新闻简报Hepatitis B is a growing concern in Ghana, with approximately 3 million people living with chronic infection. Despite this, only one per cent of cases are diagnosed, and over 500,000 individuals are infected but not receiving treatment, highlighting a significant treatment gap. The prevalence rate in the general population ranges between 8% and 12%, while among pregnant women, it is between 6.7% and 12.6%. The virus causes both acute and chronic disease, leading to severe health outcomes such as cirrhosis, liver failure, and hepatocellular carcinoma. Annually, around 2,200 new liver cancer cases are linked to chronic hepatitis B, with late-stage survival rates under 5%. The economic burden is estimated at over $100 million annually in direct medical costs and lost productivity. In response, the government has reduced treatment costs through the Global Fund’s Pooled Procurement Mechanism, and health authorities are advocating for mass screening, childhood vaccination, and public education to address the crisis. These efforts aim to prevent further infections and reduce the long-term health and economic impact of the disease.
查看新闻五要素
时间
该文章发布于2026-08-17。事件日期未明确标注,但数据涉及2024年和2025年的数据,表明该问题已持续存在。
地点与对象
该事件以加纳为中心,普通民众和孕妇受到的影响尤其严重。普通人群中的 患病率 发生率在 8% 到 12% 之间,孕妇中的发生率在 6.7% 到 12.6% 之间。
事件
Hepatitis B is described as a silent epidemic in Ghana, with approximately 3 million people living with chronic hepatitis B. The article reports that only one per cent of cases are diagnosed, and more than 500,000 people are infected but only about 20,000 are receiving treatment. It is classified as a confirmed epidemic based on the data provided.
规模、影响与风险
The scale of the epidemic is significant, with 3 million people infected and a treatment gap of over 95%. The risk includes the progression of most infected individuals to liver disease or death within two decades, as well as an annual cost of over $100 million in direct medical expenses and lost productivity. The article also notes that approximately 2,200 new cases of liver cancer are recorded annually, the majority linked to chronic hepatitis B infection.
应对、状态与不确定性
加纳卫生当局呼吁采取多管齐下的方法,包括大规模筛查活动、加速儿童 疫苗接种、扩大 抗病毒药物 治疗的覆盖范围以及公共教育。政府通过全球基金的集中采购机制确保了 治疗 成本的降低,使每位患者的年度成本降至约 48 美元。回应正在进行中,并基于所提供证据的调查结果。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Hepatitis B in Ghana: A Silent Epidemic Lurking in the Shadows - Modern Ghana

News Brief
Hepatitis B is a growing concern in Ghana, with approximately 3 million people living with chronic infection. Despite this, only one per cent of cases are diagnosed, and over 500,000 individuals are infected but not receiving treatment, highlighting a significant treatment gap. The prevalence rate in the general population ranges between 8% and 12%, while among pregnant women, it is between 6.7% and 12.6%. The virus causes both acute and chronic disease, leading to severe health outcomes such as cirrhosis, liver failure, and hepatocellular carcinoma. Annually, around 2,200 new liver cancer cases are linked to chronic hepatitis B, with late-stage survival rates under 5%. The economic burden is estimated at over $100 million annually in direct medical costs and lost productivity. In response, the government has reduced treatment costs through the Global Fund’s Pooled Procurement Mechanism, and health authorities are advocating for mass screening, childhood vaccination, and public education to address the crisis. These efforts aim to prevent further infections and reduce the long-term health and economic impact of the disease.
Original source text (English)
☰ ✕ HOME NEWS SPORTS SHOWBIZ AFRICA WORLD BUSINESS OPINIONS POLITICS LIFESTYLE HEALTH Close the sidebar NEWS HEALTH BUSINESS POLITICS OPINIONS SHOWBIZ SPORTS AFRICA WORLD Hepatitis B in Ghana: A Silent Epidemic Lurking in the Shadows By Livingstone Afenyo 17 Aug 2026 The revelation that approximately 3 million Ghanaians are living with chronic hepatitis B—with deaths rising from 15,443 in 2024 to an estimated 16,000 in 2025—has exposed a silent epidemic ravaging communities largely unnoticed. With only one per cent of cases diagnosed, this viral time bomb threatens to overwhelm Ghana’s healthcare system. Hepatitis B is a viral infection that attacks the liver and is transmitted through contact with infected blood, semen, or other body fluids. The virus causes both acute and chronic disease, with chronic infection leading to cirrhosis, liver failure, and hepatocellular carcinoma. In Ghana, the prevalence rate ranges between 8% and 12% in the general population, classifying the country as highly endemic. The true scale of the crisis is only now becoming clear. Data from the Ghana Health Service indicates that more than 500,000 people are infected, yet only about 20,000 patients are currently receiving treatment—a treatment gap of over 95%. Without intervention, most of the 3 million infected individuals will progress to liver disease or death within the next two decades, significantly increasing the national cost of care. Compounding this crisis is the alarming prevalence among pregnant women, with studies showing rates between 6.7% and 12.6%. Mother‐to‐child transmission remains a major driver of new infections, as Ghana’s hepatitis B birth‐dose vaccination programme has only recently been scaled up. Additionally, the country records approximately 2,200 new cases of liver cancer annually, the majority linked to chronic hepatitis B infection. Late‐stage survival rates are extremely low—less than 5% at five years. The economic burden is equally severe. The World Health Organization estimates that hepatitis B‐related liver disease and cancer cost Ghana more than $100 million annually in direct medical expenses and lost productivity. In response, the government has secured a significant reduction in treatment costs through the Global Fund’s Pooled Procurement Mechanism, bringing the annual cost per patient down to roughly $48. Based on these findings, health authorities are calling for a multi‐pronged approach: mass screening campaigns to identify the undiagnosed 99%, accelerated childhood vaccination, expanded access to antiviral therapy, and robust public education to combat stigma and encourage testing. Effective control of hepatitis B could save more than 10,000 lives annually, prevent 50,000 new infections each year, and free millions from the threat of liver cancer and cirrhosis—protecting both Ghana’s public health and its economic future. The response is ongoing and based on the findings from the evidence provided.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

慢性乙型肝炎 中的性功能障碍经常被忽视 - EMJ

Sexual Dysfunction in Chronic Hepatitis B Often Overlooked - EMJ
新闻简报2026 年 8 月 20 日发表的一篇叙述性评论强调,性功能障碍在 慢性乙型肝炎 成人中很常见,但未被充分认识,并且是多因素的,报告的 患病率 范围从 8.6% 到 63.1%。较高的发病率与晚期肝病、更大的纤维化和严重抑郁症有关。在接受口服 抗病毒药物 治疗的患者中,抑郁和焦虑的比例分别为 33.0% 和 38.3%。作者呼吁使用经过验证的工具和个性化管理进行每年一次或每年两次的常规筛查,以解决肝病控制、心脏代谢状况、心理支持和生活方式改变的问题。他们强调,针对 慢性乙型肝炎 的证据仍然有限,并且一些干预措施是从其他慢性病人群中推断出来的,这强调了有针对性的随机试验的必要性。
查看新闻五要素
时间
2026 年 8 月 20 日发布;事件被描述为当前且正在审查中。
地点与对象
全球 慢性乙型肝炎 感染成人人口;具体的队列研究包括肝功能较差、纤维化程度较高和严重抑郁的患者。
事件
一篇叙述性评论报告称,性功能障碍是常见的、多因素的,并且在 慢性乙型肝炎 的常规护理中经常被忽视,患病率 的估计范围为 8.6% 至 63.1%。
规模、影响与风险
慢性乙型肝炎 队列研究 中报告的性功能障碍 患病率 范围为 8.6% 至 63.1%,其中 1 个 队列研究 显示受影响率为 25.4%;较高的发病率与肝功能较差、纤维化程度较高和严重抑郁症相关。在口服抗病毒药物治疗的患者中,抑郁症达到33.0%,焦虑症达到38.3%。
应对、状态与不确定性
作者建议使用经过验证的仪器(例如国际勃起功能指数、女性性功能指数)进行年度或每年两次筛查,并进行个性化管理,以解决肝病控制、心脏代谢状况、心理支持和生活方式改变的问题。针对 慢性乙型肝炎 的具体证据仍然有限,并且一些干预措施是从其他慢性病人群中推断出来的。慢性乙型肝炎 缺乏随机试验。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Sexual Dysfunction in Chronic Hepatitis B Often Overlooked - EMJ

News Brief
A narrative review published on 20 August 2026 highlights that sexual dysfunction is common, under-recognized, and multifactorial among adults with chronic hepatitis B, with reported prevalence ranging from 8.6% to 63.1%. Higher rates are linked to advanced liver disease, greater fibrosis, and severe depression. Among patients receiving oral antiviral therapy, depression and anxiety were reported in 33.0% and 38.3% respectively. The authors call for routine annual or twice-yearly screening using validated tools and individualized management that addresses liver disease control, cardiometabolic conditions, psychological support, and lifestyle changes. They emphasize that evidence specific to chronic hepatitis B remains limited and that several interventions are extrapolated from other chronic disease populations, underscoring the need for targeted randomized trials.
Original source text (English)
Sexual Dysfunction in Chronic Hepatitis B - AMJ Therapy Area Allergy & Immunology Cardiology Dermatology Diabetes Flagship Journal Gastroenterology General Healthcare Hematology Hepatology Innovations Interventional Cardiology Microbiology & Infectious Diseases Nephrology Neurology Oncology Radiology Reproductive Health Respiratory Rheumatology Urology Podcasts Journals Webinars Congress Hub Toolkit Paediatric Growth Calculator DVT Risk Calculator Due Date Calculator Mean Arterial Pressure (MAP) Calculator BMI Calculator Collaborate About EQOH Careers Contact Us Media Pack Editorial Enquiries Contributors & Authors Submit a Paper Media Partners Pharma Partners EMJ Interactive EMJ GOLD CME search Join FREE This site is intended for healthcare professionals You must accept all conditions before being able to view the post Continue EUR USA EUR USA Therapy Area Allergy & Immunology Cardiology Dermatology Diabetes Flagship Journal Gastroenterology General Healthcare Hematology Hepatology Innovations Interventional Cardiology Microbiology & Infectious Diseases Nephrology Neurology Oncology Radiology Reproductive Health Respiratory Rheumatology Urology Podcasts Journals Webinars Congress Hub Toolkit Paediatric Growth Calculator DVT Risk Calculator Due Date Calculator Mean Arterial Pressure (MAP) Calculator BMI Calculator Collaborate About EQOH Careers Contact Us Media Pack Editorial Enquiries Contributors & Authors Submit a Paper Media Partners Pharma Partners EMJ Interactive EMJ GOLD CME Join FREE Search type Keyword Author Home Microbiology & Infectious Diseases Sexual Dysfunction in Chronic Hepatitis B Often Overlooked Sexual Dysfunction in Chronic Hepatitis B Often Overlooked 20 Aug 2026 Microbiology & Infectious Diseases View All News Key Summary: Reported sexual dysfunction prevalence ranged from 8.6% to 63.1%. Biological, psychological, and social factors may contribute. Routine screening could uncover needs missed by standard monitoring. SEXUAL dysfunction in chronic hepatitis B is common, multifactorial, and frequently missed in routine clinical care. A narrative review found prevalence estimates ranging from 8.6% to 63.1% across heterogeneous studies of hepatitis B virus related disease. In one larger chronic hepatitis B cohort, 25.4% of patients experienced sexual dysfunction, with higher rates among those with poorer liver function, greater fibrosis, and more severe depression. Sexual Dysfunction in Chronic Hepatitis B Has Multiple Drivers The review presents sexual dysfunction in chronic hepatitis B as the product of interacting biological, psychological, and social factors. Liver dysfunction may disturb sex hormone metabolism, while chronic inflammation, endothelial impairment, neuropathy, and cardiometabolic comorbidities may further affect sexual response. In men, erectile dysfunction is the most frequently studied manifestation. Women may experience reduced desire, impaired arousal or lubrication, dyspareunia, and orgasmic difficulties, although female specific evidence remains limited. Anxiety, depression, concerns about viral transmission, stigma, and relationship strain may also reduce desire and intimacy. Among patients receiving oral antiviral therapy, reported prevalence reached 33.0% for depression and 38.3% for anxiety. Antiviral Effects Require Careful Interpretation Available evidence does not show a consistent direct association between nucleos(t)ide analogues and sexual dysfunction. When symptoms develop during therapy, clinicians should assess liver disease progression, hypogonadism, psychological distress, diabetes, and other comorbidities before attributing them to treatment. Interferon based regimens may contribute indirectly through fatigue and neuropsychiatric adverse effects. However, chronic hepatitis B specific evidence remains limited. Screening Could Close a Clinical Care Gap The proposed pathway recommends annual or twice yearly screening using validated instruments, including the International Index of Erectile Function and Female Sexual Function Index. Positive results should prompt a nonjudgmental sexual history and assessment of endocrine, psychological, relational, and lifestyle factors. Management should be individualized and may include optimizing liver disease control, addressing cardiometabolic conditions, treating specific sexual symptoms, providing psychological support, and encouraging appropriate lifestyle changes. The authors emphasized that several interventions are extrapolated from other chronic disease populations because randomized trials in chronic hepatitis B are lacking. Future studies should use standardized, sex specific measures and include sexual health outcomes in antiviral trials. Reference Muzammal S et al. Sexual dysfunction in chronic hepatitis B: a comprehensive review of pathophysiology, clinical burden, and integrated management. Front Public Health. 2026;14. doi:10.3389/fpubh.2026.1865911. When symptoms develop during therapy, clinicians should assess liver disease progression, hypogonadism, psychological distress, diabetes, and other comorbidities before attributing them to treatment. Interferon based regimens may contribute indirectly through fatigue and neuropsychiatric adverse effects. However, chronic hepatitis B specific evidence remains limited. Screening Could Close a Clinical Care Gap. The proposed pathway recommends annual or twice yearly screening using validated instruments, including the International Index of Erectile Function and Female Sexual Function Index. Positive results should prompt a nonjudgmental sexual history and assessment of endocrine, psychological, relational, and lifestyle factors. Management should be individualized and may include optimizing liver disease control, addressing cardiometabolic conditions, treating specific sexual symptoms, providing psychological support, and encouraging appropriate lifestyle changes. The authors emphasized that several interventions are extrapolated from other chronic disease populations because randomized trials in chronic hepatitis B are lacking. Future studies should use standardized, sex specific measures and include sexual health outcomes in antiviral trials. Reference Muzammal S et al. Sexual dysfunction in chronic hepatitis B: a comprehensive review of pathophysiology, clinical burden, and integrated management. Front Public Health. 2026;14. doi:10.3389/fpubh.2026.1865911.
